*Ved Sriraman,Peihan Liu,Daniel Hsu,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个带噪专家模型来解释离线模仿学习（如SFT）与在线模仿学习（如OPD）在语言模型训练中的性能差异。理论分析表明：当专家策略存在噪声时，离线学习需要指数级样本复杂度才能匹敌干净专家，而在线交互式学习仅需多项式复杂度，从而为实践中在线方法优于离线方法提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习在序列决策系统中至关重要，但存在一个核心谜题：理论上离线模仿学习可以做到无视视界（horizon-free）且最优，实践中在线方法（如on-policy distillation）却常优于离线方法（如监督微调）。作者希望从理论上解释这一差距，并指导语言模型训练实践。

Method: 1. 提出带噪专家模型，假设学习者只能访问带噪版本的专家策略，但目标是竞争干净专家的奖励。2. 理论分析离线学习与在线学习的样本复杂度下界与上界。3. 提出一种改进的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）变体，证明其多项式视界依赖。4. 将结果推广到未知腐败（unknown corruption）设置（干净专家为确定性策略的情况）。

Result: 1. 离线学习带噪轨迹本质困难：样本复杂度随视界指数增长（与干净专家设置形成鲜明对比）。2. 在线交互式学习（通过新OPD变体）可达到多项式视界依赖。3. 在专家噪声分布的自然条件下（且该条件被证明是任何horizon-free样本复杂度的必要条件），可进一步获得horizon-free保证，但算法在策略类大小上牺牲统计效率。4. 提供了相应的下界，并将结果扩展到更实际的未知腐败设置。

Conclusion: 本文为在线策略蒸馏（OPD）优于监督微调（SFT）这一常见经验现象提供了严格的理论基础，解释了为何在训练语言模型（特别是长思维链任务）时，使用不完美教师的在线方法比离线方法更有效，并导出了实践中常用的替代损失函数。

Abstract: Imitation Learning is a natural framework for learning in sequential decision-making systems and has emerged as the dominant paradigm through which we understand language model training. A central puzzle is that, while in theory offline IL can be horizon-free and optimal, in practice online methods such as on-policy distillation often outperform offline methods such as supervised fine-tuning. We propose a noisy expert model to explain this gap, in which the learner only has access to a noisy version of the expert's policy, but wishes to compete against the reward achieved by a clean expert, motivated by the fact that in many applications, e.g. training language models to perform long chains of thought, the expert is often imperfect. In this setting, we show a sharp separation between offline and online IL. Offline learning from noisy trajectories is fundamentally hard: to compete with the clean expert, the sample complexity must grow exponentially, in contradistinction to the clean expert setting where no explicit horizon dependence exists. In contrast, we prove that online interaction with the noisy expert via a novel variant of OPD enables polynomial dependence on the horizon in general. We further show that, under a natural condition on the expert noise distribution, which we show to be necessary for any horizon-free sample complexity, one can obtain such a guarantee, although our proposed algorithm sacrifices statistical efficiency in its dependence on the size of the policy class. Our analysis leads to an alternative loss function that is commonly considered empirically for LM training. We further provide algorithms and lower bounds, and extend our results to the more realistic setting of unknown corruption when the clean expert is deterministic, thereby providing a theoretical foundation for why OPD can outperform SFT when training language models from imperfect teachers.

</details>


### [10] [Revocable Learned State via Process Sidecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30788)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"过程侧车"（process sidecars）方法，通过两系数编辑族来消除语言模型安全微调后残留的记忆信息，证明该方法相比朴素的任务算术编辑具有二阶精度优势，实验验证了其在拒绝回答（refusal）任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的安全微调是在公共技能学习、私有记忆阶段之后进行的，安全优化器会"弯曲"（translate）记忆方向，导致简单的记忆减法无法正确撤销记忆——因为安全阶段已经改变了记忆向量的空间位置。因此需要一种能够考虑安全训练过程影响的编辑方法。

Method: 提出过程侧车（process sidecars）编辑族：$\hatθ(λ,γ)=θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}-λΔ_{\mathrm{M}}-γ\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$，其中$\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}=\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}(Δ_{\mathrm{M}})-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$，$\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}$是通过中心割线（centered secant）近似AdamW安全训练过程的映射。实现中取$\varepsilon=1$，利用$θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}$作为正向端点，额外计算$θ_{\mathrm{A}}-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$处的安全追踪。

Result: 理论证明：(1) 精确侧车在$(λ,γ)=(1,1)$时，二阶精度恢复反事实安全仅优模型$θ_{\mathrm{AS}}$；(2) 任何标量任务算术编辑都会留下一阶反事实误差，而过程侧车编辑达到二阶精度。实验上，在三个模型上，验证集选出的二维编辑在所有试验中均优于朴素任务算术，且在配对的$γ=λ$过程-JVP子族对比中全面占优。

Conclusion: 过程信息（即安全训练如何弯曲记忆方向的信息）对于正确撤销记忆是必要的，简单的参数减法（任务算术）不足以应对安全优化器的非平凡作用。

Abstract: Language models are often adapted in stages: a public skill phase, a private memory phase, and a later safety phase that learns to refuse outputs tied to the remembered entities. Revoking the memory after the safety phase is not the same problem as subtracting the memory update: the later safety optimizer has transported the memory direction. We introduce process sidecars, a two-coefficient edit family $\hatθ(λ,γ)=θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}-λΔ_{\mathrm{M}}-γ\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$, with $\hat{R}_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}=\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}(Δ_{\mathrm{M}})-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$, where $\hat{J}_{\mathrm{S},\varepsilon}$ is a centered secant through the realized future AdamW safety-training process. The implementation uses $\varepsilon=1$ at the natural memory-edit scale; it reuses $θ_{\mathrm{AMS}}$ as the positive endpoint and computes one additional safety trace at $θ_{\mathrm{A}}-Δ_{\mathrm{M}}$. We prove two things. First, the exact sidecar, using the true transported direction $R_{\mathrm{S}\leftarrow\mathrm{M}}$ rather than the secant estimate, at $(λ,γ)=(1,1)$ recovers the counterfactual safety-only oracle $θ_{\mathrm{AS}}$ up to second order; the proof treats AdamW as an augmented-state map over parameters, first moments, and second moments. Second, this process information is necessary: whenever future safety training bends the memory direction, every scalar task-arithmetic edit leaves first-order counterfactual error, while the process-sidecar edit is second-order accurate. Across three models, the validation-selected 2D edit improves held-out refusal closure over naive task arithmetic in all trials, and over the $γ=λ$ process-JVP subfamily, the diagonal slice of the cached 2D grid, in all paired trials.

</details>


### [11] [Multistage Defer Trees for Hybrid Interpretability: If at First You Can't Succeed, Tree Again](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30995)
*Zakk Heile,Hayden McTavish,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多阶段延迟决策树（Multistage Defer Trees），通过序列化稀疏决策树处理大部分样本，仅对少数难样本延迟到黑盒模型，在保持可解释性的同时匹配集成模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策树在噪声场景下可匹配黑盒模型，但复杂集成树虽精度更高却牺牲了可解释性，导致精度-可解释性权衡难题。希望在不显著牺牲精度的情况下，最大化可解释样本的比例。

Method: 构建多阶段延迟决策树序列：每个稀疏决策树处理大部分样本并做出预测，仅将少量难样本延迟到下一阶段，最终阶段可接入黑盒模型。采用多种训练技术保持模型简洁性。

Result: 该模型在匹配复杂树集成模型性能的同时，能够将大部分样本通过仅一个或少量稀疏决策树完成分类，扩展了精度-可解释性前沿。

Conclusion: 即使复杂模型在某些场景下是必要的，也不必完全黑箱；通过多阶段延迟机制，可以在保持可解释性的同时达到先进精度，为精度-可解释性权衡提供新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that well-optimized individual decision trees can match complex black box models in some settings, primarily in noisy domains. For the remaining settings, however, complex ensembled compositions of trees often achieve higher accuracy at the cost of interpretability, leaving practitioners with difficult modeling decisions along an accuracy-interpretability tradeoff. Ideally, we would like to classify as much of the data as possible with one or a small number of trees, achieving interpretability for most samples while maintaining state-of-the-art accuracy. We introduce Multistage Defer Trees: a sequence of sparse decision trees that each make predictions for most samples, while deferring a small proportion to the next tree in the sequence or, ultimately, to a black box. We demonstrate that we can train this model class to match the performance of complex tree-based ensembles while routing most samples through only one or a small number of sparse decision trees. We discuss a range of techniques for training these models while maintaining simplicity. Our method expands the accuracy--interpretability frontier in settings where single-tree methods remain insufficient, demonstrating that even when complex models are necessary, they need not be fully opaque.

</details>


### [12] [Can Tabular In-Context Learners Generalize to Biomolecular Property Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31126)
*Davy Guan,Lu Zhang,Asiri Wijesinghe,Allen Zhu,He Zhao,Helen Power,F. Hafna Ahmed,Andrew Warden,Cheng Soon Ong,Daniel M. Steinberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了表格基础模型（如TabPFN3和TabICL）在生物分子性质预测中的应用，发现尽管这些模型基于随机因果图的合成数据预训练，但在蛋白质适应度回归和小分子分类任务中表现良好，性能高度依赖于所使用的序列或分子表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子性质预测面临标记数据有限的瓶颈，虽然预训练编码器提供了丰富的固定长度表示，但构建数据高效的少样本预测器仍是关键挑战。表格基础模型本不应适用于生物分子数据，但作者发现它们意外地有效。

Method: 将表格基础模型作为预测器-表示对进行评估，在蛋白领域使用ESMC固定表示进行适应度回归（ProteinGym和酯酶数据集），在小分子领域使用ECFP/RDKit描述符进行分类（TDC ADMET、MoleculeNet、FS-Mol、DrugOOD）。

Result: 蛋白适应度回归中，表格上下文学习一致表现优异；小分子分类中，无单一配对占优，表示选择成为主要决定因素。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型在生物分子预测任务中表现强劲，但其性能强烈依赖于所使用的序列或分子表示。

Abstract: Predicting biomolecular properties from limited labeled data is a central bottleneck in protein engineering and small-molecule design. As strong pretrained encoders now supply rich fixed-length representations, the difficulty has shifted from representation learning to building a data-efficient predictor for the few-shot regime. Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN3 and TabICL are unlikely candidates for this role: they are in-context learners pretrained on synthetic tables drawn from random causal graphs, a generative prior with no obvious correspondence to the processes that produce protein sequences or molecular graphs. That this tabular, causal inductive bias should transfer to biomolecular data at all is unintuitive, yet we find it does. Treating each method as a predictor-representation pair, we evaluate across two domains. Over a fixed ESMC representation, tabular in-context learning is consistently competitive for protein fitness regression on ProteinGym and a diverse esterase dataset. For small-molecule classification with ECFP/RDKit descriptors, no single pairing dominates across TDC ADMET, MoleculeNet, FS-Mol, and DrugOOD; representation choice becomes a primary determinant, as expected when the predictor's own prior is indifferent to molecular structure. We conclude that tabular foundation models are strong performers on biomolecular prediction tasks, but that their performance depends strongly on the sequence or molecular representation used.

</details>


### [13] [Gradient Smoothing: Coupling Layer-wise Updates for Improved Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30813)
*Haoming Meng,Anton Sugolov,Vardan Papyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度梯度增强（Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation）优化框架，并实例化为梯度平滑（Gradient Smoothing）方法。通过对各层优化器更新沿深度维度进行平滑变换（如窗口平滑），该方法在多种架构（Transformer、ViT）和训练任务（语言模型预训练、强化学习后训练、扩散模型、图像分类）中一致提升优化与泛化性能，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中不同层之间存在结构化关系，但现有优化器（SGD、Adam等）独立更新每一层，未能利用这种跨层结构信息。

Method: 提出深度梯度增强框架，将每层的优化器更新沿深度维度进行变换。具体实例化为梯度平滑方法，使用窗口平滑算子对相邻层的更新进行平滑处理，兼容任意基础优化器。

Result: 在语言模型预训练、LLM推理强化学习后训练、扩散模型、ViT图像分类等多个任务中，梯度平滑一致提升优化和泛化性能，并促进更结构化的表示演化。

Conclusion: 深度梯度增强是利用跨层结构进行优化的有效框架，梯度平滑作为其简单实例具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks with repeated architectural blocks, such as transformers, often exhibit structured relationships across layers that emerge during training. Motivated by this observation, we introduce \emph{Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation}, a general optimization paradigm in which the update applied to each layer is obtained by transforming the collection of block-wise optimizer updates along the depth dimension. Within this framework, we study \emph{Gradient Smoothing}, a family of depth-wise smoothing methods, and instantiate it with a simple local \emph{Window Smoothing} operator. The resulting method operates directly on block-wise updates produced by arbitrary base optimizers (e.g., SGD, Adam, Muon), incurs minimal computational overhead, and is compatible with existing optimization pipelines. We evaluate Gradient Smoothing across a diverse set of architectures and training regimes, including language model pretraining, RL post-training of LLMs for reasoning, diffusion modeling, and image classification with Vision Transformers. Across these settings, Gradient Smoothing consistently improves optimization and generalization performance without modifying model architectures or training objectives. We further show that it promotes more structured representation evolution across depth, consistent with its interpretation as a structured depth-wise preconditioning method. Together, these results establish Depth-wise Gradient Augmentation as a promising framework for exploiting cross-depth structure in optimization and demonstrate Gradient Smoothing as a simple and broadly applicable instantiation.

</details>


### [14] [Learning Gaussian Graphical Models from a Glauber Trajectory Without Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31230)
*Eric Shen,Tony Wu,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从单条Glauber动力学轨迹中学习d-稀疏高斯图模型结构的问题，提出了一种多项式时间算法，其轨迹长度保证不依赖于混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际应用中观测数据存在时间相关性而非独立同分布，且经典独立同分布场景下亚线性样本复杂度在多项式时间内难以实现，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 算法包含三个组件：1) 估计条件方差并重新缩放轨迹以简化为单位对角情况；2) 设计局部边测试，通过隔离成对影响从短更新窗口中提取邻接信息；3) 使用基于中位数的鲁棒估计器聚合局部统计量，克服单条轨迹的时间依赖性。

Result: 该算法能从单条Glauber动力学轨迹中恢复条件独立图，且轨迹长度保证不依赖于混合时间。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一个在时间相关观测下学习高斯图模型结构的有效算法，解决了经典独立同分布场景中多项式时间算法的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the task of learning the structure of a $d$-sparse Gaussian graphical model on $n$ variables from a single trajectory of Glauber dynamics. Beyond algorithmic considerations, many applications present temporally correlated observations rather than i.i.d.\ samples. In the classical i.i.d.\ setting, under comparably general sparsity and minimum edge-strength assumptions, sublinear-in-$n$ sample guarantees are known, but achieving them in polynomial-time remains open. Motivated in part by this gap, we give a polynomial-time algorithm that recovers the conditional-independence graph from a single Glauber trajectory, with a trajectory-length guarantee that does not depend on the mixing time.
  Technically, our algorithm has three components. First, we estimate the conditional variances and rescale the trajectory to reduce to the unit-diagonal case, without changing the underlying graph. Second, we design a local edge test that extracts adjacency information from short update windows by isolating pairwise influence. Third, we aggregate these local statistics using a robust median-based estimator, and prove accuracy despite temporal dependence arising from a single trajectory.

</details>


### [15] [Mind the Residual Gap: Probabilistic Downscaling under Real-World Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30821)
*Yujin Kim,Nidhi Soma,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ReMatch方法，通过最优传输在PCA空间中对齐训练与测试的残差分布，解决了概率降尺度中均值-残差范式因残差目标错配导致的偏差和欠分散问题，在合成基准和真实HRRR-ERA5风场降尺度任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有均值-残差范式在实际应用中常产生有偏和欠分散的集合，作者发现根本原因不是简单的预测不确定性校准问题，而是**残差目标错配**——训练时诱导的残差分布与测试时所需的残差分布因降尺度偏差而系统性不同。

Method: 提出ReMatch（残差分布匹配）方法：通过最优传输在低维PCA空间中将训练残差分布对齐到测试时分布，从而保持均值-残差框架的统计优势，同时减少随机生成器所见残差目标的训练-测试不匹配。

Result: 在受控合成基准（不同偏差水平）和真实HRRR-ERA5风场降尺度任务上，ReMatch显著减少了欠分散，改善了校准指标（SSR和CRPS），并优于标准均值-残差模型、其变体以及最先进的超分辨率模型。

Conclusion: ReMatch通过分布匹配有效解决了残差目标错配这一根本问题，提升了概率降尺度的校准性能。

Abstract: Probabilistic downscaling is the task of modeling the conditional distribution of high-resolution fields given coarse inputs, and is a central challenge to atmospheric science, climate modeling, and other multiscale physical systems. A widely used paradigm decomposes the problem into a deterministic mean predictor followed by a stochastic residual generator. While effective in idealized settings, this mean--residual approach frequently produces biased and under-dispersive ensembles in real-world applications. Is this merely generic predictive uncertainty miscalibration? We show that the root cause is more fundamental: residual target misspecification, the residual distribution induced during training differs systematically from the one required at test time due to downscaling bias. To close this gap, we introduce ReMatch (Residual Distribution Matching). ReMatch aligns the training residual distribution toward the test-time regime via optimal transport in a low-dimensional PCA space. This preserves the statistical benefits of the mean--residual framework while reducing the train--test mismatch in the residual targets seen by the stochastic generator. On a controlled synthetic benchmark with varying bias levels and a real-world HRRR--ERA5 wind field downscaling task, ReMatch substantially reduces under-dispersion, improves calibration (SSR and CRPS), and outperforms strong baselines, including the standard mean--residual model and its variants, as well as state-of-the-art super-resolution models. Our code is available at https://github.com/sdean-group/ReMatch.git.

</details>


### [16] [Sequential sparse Gaussian process quantile regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31284)
*Hugo Nicolas,Olivier Le Maître*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于稀疏高斯过程的贝叶斯分位数回归框架，利用拉普拉斯近似进行后验推断，并通过预测不确定性分解驱动诱导点填充和数据采集两种自适应机制，在基准问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯高斯过程分位数回归虽然能提供不确定量化，但非共轭的非对称拉普拉斯似然和后验推断计算成本极高，需要开发高效的计算框架。

Method: 使用稀疏高斯过程通过少量诱导变量表示分位函数，采用拉普拉斯近似进行后验推断；将预测不确定分解为条件先验方差和后验诱导方差两部分，驱动诱导点填充和数据采集两种互补自适应机制；结合为顺序算法动态分配计算资源。

Result: 基准数值实验表明：拉普拉斯近似具有准确性，基于方差的诱导点放置策略优于已有方法，所提出的顺序富集策略相比预定义数据采集策略更有效。

Conclusion: 所提出的稀疏高斯过程框架能够有效降低贝叶斯分位数回归的计算负担，同时通过自适应机制实现模型复杂度的动态控制。

Abstract: Quantile regression aims to estimate the conditional quantiles of a response variable from observed data. In a Bayesian setting, Gaussian process quantile regression provides uncertainty quantification but faces significant computational challenges due to the nonconjugacy of the asymmetric Laplace likelihood and the cost of posterior inference. We develop a sparse Gaussian process framework in which the quantile function is represented through a reduced set of inducing variables and posterior inference is performed using a Laplace approximation. A decomposition of the predictive uncertainty into conditional-prior and posterior-induced variance components is then exploited to drive two complementary adaptive mechanisms: inducing-input infilling and data acquisition. These mechanisms are combined within a sequential algorithm that allocates computational effort toward the dominant source of predictive uncertainty and adaptively controls model complexity. Numerical experiments on benchmark problems demonstrate the accuracy of the Laplace approximation, the benefits of variance-based inducing-input placement, and the effectiveness of the proposed sequential enrichment strategy compared with predefined data-acquisition strategies.

</details>


### [17] [Partition-Guided Distance Saliency: Bridging Decision and Objective Spaces in Many-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30836)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Flávio Vinícius Cruzeiro Martins,Elizabeth Fialho Wanner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多目标优化中高维Pareto前沿可解释性差的问题，提出PGDS框架，通过替代模型学习决策空间到目标空间的几何距离映射、自动划分目标区域并识别局部支配点、以及基于扰动距离变化的特征敏感性分析，将决策变量分类为"驱动者"或"阻碍者"，在10目标基准和焊接梁工程问题上验证了其提供差异化可操作洞察的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着目标数量增加，Pareto前沿复杂度急剧上升，传统可视化方法失效，决策者面临"认知枯竭"，难以理解高维决策变量与目标结果之间的关系，也缺乏先验知识来指定目标区域或识别相关权衡。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 构建替代模型，学习决策空间几何距离到目标空间接近度的映射；(2) 自动划分目标景观为不同区域，识别局部"支配点"作为自动目标；(3) 通过扰动每个决策变量测量距离偏移，量化解的敏感性，将特征分类为"驱动者"（促进向偏好区域收敛）或"阻碍者"（阻碍进一步改进的几何约束）。

Result: 在10目标基准问题和焊接梁工程问题上，PGDS提供了传统可视化和基于规则的XAI方法无法提供的差异化、可操作洞察。

Conclusion: PGDS框架通过几何直觉驱动的自动化解释流程，有效桥接了多目标优化的可解释性鸿沟，为高维连续优化场景提供了实用的特征归因分析工具。

Abstract: Explainability in Many-Objective Optimization (MaO) is currently hindered by the escalating complexity of the Pareto front, which renders the relationship between high-dimensional decision variables and objective outcomes increasingly opaque. As the number of objectives exceeds the limits of traditional visualization, decision-makers encounter a ``cognitive drought'' in identifying relevant trade-offs or specifying target regions without a priori knowledge. To bridge this interpretability gap, we introduce the {Partition-Guided Distance Saliency (PGDS)} framework, a novel XAI approach designed for continuous optimization landscapes. Our framework automates the explanation process through a three-stage pipeline that prioritizes geometric intuition over abstract rules. First, we employ a surrogate model that learns how geometric distances in the decision space map to proximity in the objective space. Second, to address the difficulty of manual target selection in high dimensions, the framework automatically partitions the objective landscape into distinct regions and identifies local ``Dominating Points'' to serve as automated targets for improvement. Third, we quantify how sensitive a solution's position is to each decision variable by measuring the distance shifts induced by perturbations to each variable. This allows PGDS to categorize features as either ``Drivers'' which facilitate convergence toward preferred regions, or ``Blockers'' which represent geometric constraints hindering further progress. Validation on 10-objective benchmarks and a physics-informed engineering problem (Welded Beam) demonstrates that PGDS provides differentiated, actionable insights that traditional visualization and rule-based XAI methods fail to provide.

</details>


### [18] [Contextual Slate GLM Bandits with Limited Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31449)
*Tanmay Goyal,Sukruta Prakash Midigeshi,Gaurav Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限自适应下的上下文石板赌博机问题（广义线性奖励），提出了两种算法：B-SlateGLinCB（批处理设置，将时间划分为O(log log T)个批次）和RS-SlateGLinCB（罕见切换设置，仅进行O(Nd log T)次参数更新）。在物品序列多样性假设下，分别实现了O(Nd^{3/2}√T)和O(Nd√T)的遗憾界，且均不依赖于非线参数κ。算法计算高效，每轮仅需poly(N)时间。实验表明算法优于有限自适应基线，与全自适应SOTA算法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文石板赌博机问题在实践中广泛存在（如推荐系统），但现有算法通常需要完全自适应（每轮更新），这在通信成本高或计算受限的场景下不可行。本文旨在探索有限自适应场景下是否仍能实现高效的遗憾界，并突破GLM遗憾界中对非线参数κ的依赖。

Method: 针对两种有限自适应场景分别提出B-SlateGLinCB（批处理）和RS-SlateGLinCB（罕见切换）。批处理算法将时间轴划分为O(log log T)个批次，每个批次仅依赖之前批次的数据；罕见切换算法自适应地仅在必要时进行参数更新（共O(Nd log T)次）。两算法均基于广义线性模型和上置信界（UCB）框架，并利用石板结构实现计算高效性（避免指数级石板枚举）。

Result: B-SlateGLinCB遗憾界为O(Nd^{3/2}√T)，RS-SlateGLinCB为O(Nd√T)，均不包含非线参数κ因子（打破了GLM bandit的典型遗憾下界）。计算复杂度每轮为poly(N)，避免了2^{Ω(N)}的枚举。实验表明两算法在有限自适应场景下优于基线，且B-SlateGLinCB经简单修改后与全自适应SOTA算法性能持平。

Conclusion: 本文证明了有限自适应下石板GLM赌博机问题可实现与全自适应算法相媲美的遗憾界，且消除了κ依赖。所提算法计算高效，并在语言模型的上下文示例选择任务上展现了实用价值。这为实际部署（如大规模推荐系统、LLM提示优化）提供了理论保障和高效解决方案。

Abstract: We investigate the contextual slate bandit problem with generalized linear rewards under limited adaptivity. At each round, the learner is presented with $N$ sets of items, where each item is represented by a $d$-dimensional feature vector. The learner then constructs a slate by selecting one item per set; the resulting slate yields a scalar reward sampled from a Generalized Linear Model (GLM). We propose algorithms under two limited-adaptivity settings: (a) Batched and (b) Rarely-Switching. For the batched setting, we introduce B-SlateGLinCB, which partitions the time horizon into $\mathcal{O}(\log\log T)$ batches such that each batch's policy relies only on data from previous batches. For the rarely-switching setting, we propose RS-SlateGLinCB, which adaptively performs only $\mathcal{O}(Nd\log T)$ parameter updates. Under a diversity assumption on the item sequences, we prove that B-SlateGLinCB and RS-SlateGLinCB achieve regret bounds of $\mathcal{O}(Nd^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$ and $\mathcal{O}(Nd\sqrt{T})$, respectively. Notably, both bounds are independent of the non-linearity parameter $κ$ that is typically found to scale the regret of GLM bandit algorithms. Our algorithms are computationally efficient, requiring only $\text{poly}(N)$ time per round despite $2^{Ω(N)}$ possible slates. Simulations show our algorithms outperform existing baselines with limited adaptivity and remain competitive with Slate-GLM-OFU, a fully adaptive state-of-the-art algorithm. Notably, a slightly modified B-SlateGLinCB empirically matches this baseline. Finally, we demonstrate strong performance in a practical in-context example selection task for language models.

</details>


### [19] [On the Convergence of Self-Improving Online LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31524)
*Xudong Wu,Pangpang Liu,Vaneet Aggarwal,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAIL-RevKL算法，通过反向KL散度正则化改进SAIL的优化景观，证明其满足PL条件并具有全局收敛保证，在MuJoCo和LLM对齐任务上优于原始SAIL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始SAIL算法存在关键理论缺陷：其目标函数因Hessian矩阵的不良性质无法保证强凹性，缺乏正式的收敛性分析。

Method: 提出SAIL-RevKL，在原始目标中引入反向KL散度惩罚项进行正则化，将双层优化问题转化为高效的单层方法，并证明该正则化目标在有界参数空间内满足Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)条件。

Result: 建立了全局收敛保证，实现了近线性的样本复杂度。在MuJoCo基准测试和LLM对齐任务上，SAIL-RevKL均优于原始SAIL。

Conclusion: 通过正则化方式有效解决了SAIL的优化理论缺陷，为自改进对齐算法提供了坚实的收敛性理论基础。

Abstract: The Self-Improving Alignment (SAIL) algorithm addresses distribution shift by reducing a bilevel formulation of the problem to an efficient, single-level method. Empirically, SAIL has demonstrated strong performance on this task. However, a formal analysis of its convergence properties has been lacking. We identify a key theoretical challenge: the standard SAIL objective function is not guaranteed to be strongly concave due to unfavorable properties of its Hessian. To address this limitation, we propose a regularized objective, SAIL-RevKL, which incorporates a reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalty to improve the optimization landscape. Our central theoretical contribution is to prove that this regularized objective satisfies the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition within a bounded parameter space. We establish global convergence guarantees, achieving a near-linear sample complexity. We further validate the effectiveness and stability of SAIL-RevKL through empirical evaluations, demonstrating that it outperforms the vanilla SAIL on both MuJoCo benchmarks and LLM alignment tasks.

</details>


### [20] [A Transferable Learned Temporal Prior for Transmission Reconstruction and Decision-Relevant Uncertainty in Real Outbreak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30842)
*Md Ahsan Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了疫情传播重建中流行病学时间标度和传播标签的不确定性。通过在11个疾病家族上训练锁定的逻辑回归时间先验，在29个ANDV任务上达到MRR 0.571、Top-1准确率37.9%，显著优于基线。75对mpox系统发育一致性审计发现54.67%的传播对在基因组上未解决或不支持。保留不确定边会改变优先干预的源病例集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疫情传播重建方法将流行病学时间和传播标签视为确定性真实值，但这两者从未被系统评估过其不确定性。

Method: 1) 在11个疾病家族上训练逻辑回归时间先验（锁定参数后不再接触目标疫情数据）；2) 在29个ANDV父级排名基准任务上无重拟合应用；3) 对75对纽约mpox人际传播对进行系统发育一致性审计作为独立的标签可靠性证据。

Result: 1) 锁定先验MRR 0.571 vs 基线0.274，Top-1准确率37.9% vs 13.8%（p≤0.0002）；2) 54.67%的mpox传播对在基因组上未解决或不支持（95% CI: 42.75-66.21%）；3) 保留不确定边使ANDV和广东Delta图的top-5源优先集的Jaccard系数为0.429-0.667。

Conclusion: 传播标签的不确定性在疫情证据模块中是可测量的，保留不确定链接会改变优先干预的源病例选择，因此传播重建应系统考虑不确定性而非将其视为确定真实值。

Abstract: Outbreak transmission reconstruction treats epidemiological timing and transmission labels as deterministic ground truth; neither has been systematically evaluated. We trained a logistic regression temporal prior on eleven disease families, locked all parameters before accessing any target outbreak data, and applied it without refitting to a strict Andes virus (ANDV) parent-ranking benchmark of 29 tasks. The locked prior achieved mean reciprocal rank (MRR) 0.571 versus 0.274 and Top-1 accuracy 37.9% versus 13.8% against the best source-trained parametric baseline (permutation p <= 0.0002; 7-8 reversals to lose MRR significance). A phylogenetic concordance audit of 75 NYC mpox inter-host pairs - independent label-reliability evidence rather than a prior validation - found that 54.67% (exact 95% CI: 42.75-66.21%) were genomically unresolved or unsupported. Retaining uncertain edges in ANDV and Guangdong Delta graphs shifted top-5 source-priority sets (Jaccard 0.429-0.667). Transmission-label uncertainty was measurable in the outbreak evidence modules examined, and retaining uncertain links changed which source cases were prioritized for intervention.

</details>


### [21] [Policy Optimization Achieves Data-Dependent Regret Bounds in MDPs with Unknown Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31769)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于乐观跟随正则化领导者的策略优化算法，首次在未知转移核的在线情节式表格马尔可夫决策过程中实现了最优双边保证及数据依赖遗憾界，包括一阶、二阶和路径长度界，同时达成随机场景下与间隙相关的对数级遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明策略优化在已知转移核下能自适应对抗性和随机性损失并获得数据依赖遗憾界，但未知转移核场景下这类保证是否可实现仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出基于乐观跟随正则化领导者（OFTRL）的新算法，核心包括：1) 乐观Q函数估计器的新设计；2) 通过损失预测误差控制估计器偏差的数据依赖转移奖励项；3) 识别并纳入转移依赖复杂度项。

Result: 在未知转移核下首次实现一阶、二阶和路径长度遗憾界，同时包含转移依赖复杂度项；在随机场景下达到与间隙相关的多项式对数级遗憾。

Conclusion: 证明了策略优化在未知转移核下也能获得数据依赖遗憾保证，转移依赖复杂度项是估计转移核的固有代价，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for online episodic tabular Markov decision processes with unknown transition kernels, aiming for best-of-both-worlds guarantees together with data-dependent regret bounds. Recent work (Dann et al., 2023; Li et al., 2026) has shown that policy optimization can adapt to both adversarial and stochastic losses with first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds, but only under known transitions, leaving open whether such data-dependent guarantees are achievable by policy optimization when the transition kernel is unknown. We resolve this by developing a new algorithm based on optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader that attains these guarantees under unknown transitions. The key ingredient is a new design of optimistic $Q$-function estimators together with a data-dependent transition bonus that controls estimator bias through the loss-prediction error. Our analysis further identifies an unavoidable transition-dependent complexity term that captures the intrinsic cost of estimating the transition kernel. As a result, we obtain first-order, second-order, and path-length bounds with the transition-dependent complexity term while simultaneously achieving gap-dependent $\mathrm{polylog}(T)$ regret in the stochastic regime.

</details>


### [22] [Relational and Sequential Conformal Inference for Energy Time Series over Graphs via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31804)
*Keivan Faghih Niresi,Alice Cicirello,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STOIC框架，将图神经网络点预测与基于表格基础模型的共形预测相结合，实现空间-时间序列的不确定性量化，在五个能源数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源需求点预测方法虽准，但缺乏不确定性估计；而现有共形预测方法未能充分捕捉能源系统的复杂时空结构。

Method: STOIC由STGNN生成点预测，将时空残差重构为表格表示，利用表格基础模型通过上下文学习进行零样本校准，生成具有统计覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在五个基准数据集（含合成模拟、真实电力和区域供热网络）上，STOIC一致优于现有共形预测基线，提供更可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: STOIC有效结合了图结构建模与表格基础模型的上下文学习能力，为复杂图结构能源时间序列提供了更鲁棒的不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: Accurate energy demand forecasting is essential for the reliable operation and planning of modern sustainable energy systems. Spatial-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have recently achieved strong performance in point forecasting by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and relational dependencies across interconnected energy nodes. However, in real-world energy systems, accurate point forecasts alone are insufficient, as operators also require reliable uncertainty estimates to support risk-aware decision-making, grid stability, and operational planning under uncertainty. Conformal prediction provides a principled and model-agnostic framework for uncertainty quantification with statistical coverage guarantees, making it particularly attractive for safety-critical energy applications. However, existing conformal prediction approaches often fail to fully capture the complex spatial-temporal structure of energy systems. To address these limitations, we propose STOIC (Spatial-Temporal Graph Conformal Prediction with In-Context Learning), a novel framework that integrates graph-based forecasting with the zero-shot calibration capabilities of tabular foundation models. STOIC first generates point forecasts using an STGNN and subsequently reformulates spatial-temporal residuals into a tabular representation suitable for in-context learning. Leveraging a tabular foundation model, STOIC calibrates prediction intervals without task-specific retraining, effectively capturing both sequential and relational dependencies. We evaluate STOIC on five diverse benchmarks, including synthetic simulations as well as real-world electricity and district heating networks. Across all datasets, STOIC consistently outperforms existing conformal prediction baselines, delivering more reliable and robust uncertainty estimates for complex graph-structured energy time series.

</details>


### [23] [Personalizing Marketplace Policies with Competing Objectives and Constrained Experiments: Evidence from a Job Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30932)
*Yufei Wu,Zhen Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对双边市场（求职平台）中雇主与求职者利益冲突的挑战，提出了一套集成框架，用于个性化设置免费价值门槛政策。该框架通过混合排名模型、处理效应外推方法及生产部署，在满足参与度约束的前提下，显著提升了目标指标，并解决了标准 uplift 方法因跨边外部性和市场干扰而失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边市场中不同用户群体利益常相互冲突，改善一方体验可能损害另一方。现有标准 uplift 方法因跨边外部性（需多目标优化）和市场干扰（需聚类级随机化，导致处理水平离散、违反正性假设）而不足以解决该问题。

Method: 提出三组件集成框架：1) 基于集成学习的混合排名模型，分别建模目标和约束指标，相比单目标方法在等价目标增益下降低10%以上约束风险；2) 处理效应外推方法，利用单调性假设将有限实验变化扩展到未测试的政策水平；3) 生产部署，上线数据验证外推准确性和约束合规性。

Result: 个性化政策在目标指标上取得统计显著且经济可观的正向提升，同时满足参与度约束。混合排名模型在同等目标增益下降低约束风险超过10%，上线数据证实了处理效应外推的准确性及约束合规性。

Conclusion: 即便在实验条件严重受限、不同目标相互竞争的常见现实市场环境中，原则性的方法论仍能实现有意义的个性化策略。

Abstract: Two-sided marketplaces connect distinct user groups whose interests often conflict -- improving outcomes on one side could degrade the other side's experience. To address this challenge, we deploy an integrated framework for personalizing free-value thresholds -- a policy governing the scope of complimentary services for job listings -- across a two-sided job marketplace connecting millions of employers and job seekers. Our personalized policy delivers statistically significant and economically sizable lift in the target metric while respecting engagement guardrail constraints.
  Direct application of standard uplift methods proves insufficient here for two reasons. First, cross-side externalities demand multi-objective optimization: maximizing employer-side metrics risks harming job seeker engagement, with effects varying substantially across job segments. Second, marketplace interference necessitates cluster-level randomization, limiting us to few discrete treatment levels -- effectively a form of positivity violation that rules out methods designed for continuous treatments.
  We contribute an integrated framework with three components. Our ensemble-based hybrid ranking models target and guardrail metrics separately, cutting guardrail risk by over 10% for equivalent target gains compared to single-objective approaches. A treatment effect extrapolation method extends our estimates from limited experimental variation to untested policy levels, relying on monotonicity assumptions that we validate empirically. Finally, we present production deployment, where post-launch data confirms both extrapolation accuracy and guardrail compliance.
  Our deployed system demonstrates that principled methodology can enable meaningful personalization even when experiments are severely constrained and different objectives compete -- common conditions that characterize many real-world marketplaces.

</details>


### [24] [Signed-Permutation Coordinate Transport for RMSNorm Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31963)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明LLM残差流存在与架构相关的规范群（gauge），RMSNorm模型具有符号置换规范群B_d，并引入符号边缘化匈牙利匹配算法；实验表明坐标保持传输（而非函数级合并）能显著提升跨运行坐标对齐（91.1% vs 60.3%），且规范对齐是SAE重建、情感/拒绝引导等技术可迁移的必要条件；AdamW状态传输和可解释性审计进一步揭示规范敏感性的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM工作流需要跨检查点传输坐标索引对象（如引导向量、稀疏自编码器、top-k神经元集、归因列表和合并对齐），但这些操作仅在固定模型残差流规范（gauge）后才能良好定义。现有方法未意识到规范选择依赖于架构（LayerNorm vs RMSNorm），导致对称性不完整和工具迁移失败。

Method: 1. 理论证明RMSNorm具有符号置换规范B_d（而LayerNorm仅S_d）；2. 提出符号边缘化匈牙利匹配算法；3. 引入坐标保持传输（coordinate-preserving transport）作为主要对象，沿基础微调轨迹组合局部B_d规范；4. 实验验证规范对齐对多项任务的影响。

Result: 坐标保持传输恢复91.1%跨运行坐标（vs 端点匹配60.3%）；TinyLlama SAE重建NMSE 0.004（B_d）vs 1.08（S_d）；Qwen情感引导保留95.8%效果（vs 17.2%）；拒绝引导在S_d下符号反转；AdamW状态传输在符号置换下保持轨迹，置换仅状态则不同。

Conclusion: LLM的索引级可解释性主张仅相对于显式规范（explicit gauge）才可复现，规范对齐是跨运行工具迁移和训练的基石。

Abstract: Modern LLM workflows move coordinate-indexed objects across checkpoints: steering vectors, sparse autoencoders, top-$k$ neuron sets, attribution lists, and merge alignments. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \ltimes \{\pm 1\}^d$. Permutation-only alignment is therefore symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm models. We introduce sign-marginalized Hungarian matching and prove a sharp failure mode: with decorrelated coordinates, raw signed-correlation matching has a structural permutation-accuracy ceiling at the positive-sign fraction of the true gauge, which sign-marginalization removes. We then make coordinate-preserving transport, not function-level merging, the primary object: composing saved-checkpoint local $B_d$ gauges along same-base fine-tuning trajectories recovers 91.1% of cross-run coordinates at 1500 steps versus 60.3% for endpoint matching, and the gain is not explained by merely routing through the base. The recovered gauge transfers tools that permutation-only alignment breaks: TinyLlama SAE reconstruction has NMSE 0.004 under $B_d$ versus 1.08 under $S_d$; Qwen sentiment steering preserves 95.8% of its effect versus 17.2%; refusal steering reverses sign under $S_d$; coordinate-preserving merges behave the same way. The same covariance governs stateful training: signed transport of AdamW state preserves the resumed trajectory, while permutation-only state follows a different one from a functionally identical checkpoint. Finally, gauge-sweep audits show index-level interpretability claims are reproducible only relative to an explicit gauge.

</details>


### [25] [Quality-Aware Modulation for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30934)
*Luke Budny,Yuhong Guo,Kevin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出质量表征模块(QRM)，一种轻量级transformer模块，通过学习质量感知表征来调节DiT中的自适应LayerNorm调制，从而提升图像生成质量，且不改变采样调度或扩散主干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型（如DiT）仅依赖时间步或提示嵌入来调节去噪强度，缺乏质量感知信息，导致生成图像不对齐、视觉不一致且保真度不足。

Method: 提出QRM，一种轻量级transformer模块，基于现有模型输入学习质量感知表征，生成向量集合M_qrm，通过调整DiT transformer块内的自适应LayerNorm调制，将质量敏感信号注入去噪参数中。

Result: 消融实验验证了QRM训练损失和架构的有效性，实证结果显示在DiT基线上实现了持续的图像质量提升。

Conclusion: QRM能够在不对采样调度或扩散主干网络做显著改动的情况下，有效提升DiT模型的图像生成质量。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image diffusion models, such as diffusion transformers (DiT), rely on timestep or prompt embeddings to modulate the strength of the denoising process in each timestep. While this modulation communicates the current noise level, it does not provide any quality-aware information, which can lead to generated images that are unaligned, visually inconsistent, and lacking in fidelity. In this paper, we propose the Quality Representation Module (QRM), a lightweight transformer module that learns a quality-aware representation based on existing model inputs, and produces a set of vectors $M_{qrm}$. These vectors adjust the adaptive LayerNorm modulation within the DiT transformer blocks, thereby injecting a quality-sensitive signal into the denoising parameters. The QRM introduces no significant changes to the sampling schedule or diffusion backbone. Experiments include ablations on QRM training losses and architectures, as well as empirical results demonstrating consistent image quality improvements over baseline DiT-based models.

</details>


### [26] [Physics-informed Conditional Normalizing Flows for Angles-only Cislunar Orbit Determination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30936)
*Walther Litteri,Massimiliano Vasile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将生成模型扩展至地月空间环境中的轨道确定问题，利用归一化流从短弧角测量数据中推断初始状态的后验分布，并通过非线性最小二乘优化得到竞争性的热启动解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地月空间环境中，仅依赖短弧角测量进行轨道确定是一个具有挑战性的问题，传统方法对初始猜测敏感且难以处理多模态分布。本文旨在利用生成模型来学习测量与状态间的复杂条件分布，从而提供物理一致的统计假设。

Method: 将轨道确定问题建模为条件密度估计，使用归一化流（Normalising Flow）在近直线晕轨道（NRHO）的扰动顶心测量数据上训练，以学习灵活且可能多模态的后验分布。新测量到来时，从学习到的密度中采样获得状态假设，再通过非线性最小二乘优化进行精化。

Result: 训练好的归一化流能够生成统计一致且物理信息驱动的状态假设，经非线性最小二乘精化后，为经典算法提供了具有竞争力的热启动初始值。

Conclusion: 生成式天体动力学在短弧角测量轨道确定问题中展现出潜力，归一化流结合经典优化能够有效提升地月空间轨道确定的性能。

Abstract: Generative Astrodynamics is advanced in this work by extending generative modelling to an orbit determination problem in the cislunar environment. The task is formulated as conditional density estimation, aiming to infer the probability distribution of the initial state from angles-only measurements over short observation arcs. A normalising flow is trained on perturbed topocentric observations from Near Rectilinear Halo Orbits, enabling a flexible and potentially multimodal posterior representation. Given new measurements, the learned density is sampled to generate statistically consistent and physics-informed state hypotheses. These estimates are refined via nonlinear least-squares minimisation, providing a competitive warm start for classical algorithms.

</details>


### [27] [Constrained Online Convex Optimization without Slater's Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31480)
*Kihyun Yu,Junehee Lee,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合自适应正则化的 anytime 原始-对偶框架，用于解决带随机或对抗约束的在线凸优化问题。该框架无需 Slater 条件即可实现近乎最优的遗憾和约束违反界限，在凸损失下达到 O(√T) 期望遗憾和 O(√T log T) 期望累积约束违反，强凸损失下提升至 O(log T)，并支持高概率保证和对抗约束场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机约束在线凸优化算法通常依赖 Slater 条件等正则性假设才能达到近乎最优的遗憾和约束违反界限，而对抗约束算法则通过使用较为严格的轮次可行比较器来避免这些假设。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提出一种无需 Slater 条件、同时适用于随机和对抗约束的统一框架。

Method: 提出一种 anytime 原始-对偶框架，在 dual 更新中引入自适应正则化器（adaptive regularizer）。该正则化器能够稳定对偶过程，无需依赖 Slater 条件带来的负漂移。对于随机约束，算法在凸损失和强凸损失下分别分析；对于对抗约束，通过轻微修改框架即可提供硬约束违反保证。

Result: 对于随机约束和凸损失：O(√T) 期望遗憾和 O(√T log T) 期望累积约束违反，且具有相同阶的高概率界；对于强凸损失：遗憾和约束违反均提升至 O(log T)；对于对抗约束：通过轻微修改即可提供硬约束违反保证。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了随机约束与对抗约束在线凸优化之间的差距，提出的自适应正则化原始-对偶框架在不依赖 Slater 条件的情况下实现了近乎最优的理论保证，并为两种约束场景提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: We study constrained online convex optimization with adversarial losses and stochastic or adversarial constraints. For stochastic constraints, existing algorithms that achieve nearly optimal regret and constraint violation bounds typically rely on regularity assumptions such as Slater's condition, while adversarial-constraint algorithms avoid these assumptions by using a rather restrictive round-wise feasible comparator. We bridge this gap with an anytime primal-dual framework that incorporates an adaptive regularizer into the dual update. The regularizer stabilizes the dual process without relying on the negative drift induced by Slater's condition. For stochastic constraints and convex losses, our algorithm achieves $O(\sqrt{T})$ expected regret and $O(\sqrt{T}\log T)$ expected cumulative constraint violation. Furthermore, we show that our algorithm also admits high-probability bounds of the same order on regret and constraint violation. For strongly convex losses, the regret bound improves to $O(\log T)$ with a violation bound of the same order. With a minor modification, the framework also applies to adversarial constraints and provides guarantees for hard constraint violation.

</details>


### [28] [Estimating Supply Incrementality in Two-sided Marketplaces: A Causal Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30999)
*Yufei Wu,Daniel Schmierer,Dan Zylberglejd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了两侧市场（以Airbnb为例）中额外供给对交易量的因果影响，提出了一种结合双重/去偏机器学习与层次贝叶斯框架的因果机器学习方法，利用地理空间相似度构建特征，并验证了模型在估计市场供给回报和样本外表现上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异质性产品的两侧市场中，理解额外供给与市场结果（如交易量或交易额）之间的因果关系至关重要，但这一关系难以准确估计。

Method: 结合双重/去偏机器学习（Double/Debiased Machine Learning）与层次贝叶斯框架，利用地理空间文献中的产品段相似度度量构建可处理且信息丰富的特征，用于因果估计。

Result: 该模型能够提供关于额外供给对市场回报的合理估计，并展现出良好的样本外预测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的因果机器学习方法可有效估计两侧市场中不同产品段的供给弹性，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: In two-sided marketplaces with heterogeneous products, it is important to understand the causal relationship between additional supply and marketplace outcomes, such as the total quantity transacted or transaction value in the marketplace. This paper studies a causal machine learning approach to estimating this relationship across product segments. We use the Airbnb marketplace as an example, focusing on the impact of additional listing supply on total bookings, but the methodology applies to other two-sided marketplaces. Our approach combines double/debiased machine learning with a hierarchical Bayesian framework that leverages pre-existing knowledge as priors. We construct tractable and informative features for the model by leveraging measures of product segment similarity from the geospatial literature. We find that such a model provides plausible estimates of the marketplace returns to additional supply and strong out of sample performance.

</details>


### [29] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Fluid Controls: Data-based Multi-observational Policy Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31025)
*Deepak Akhare,Luning Sun,Xin-Yang Liu,Xiantao Fan,Timo Bremer,Ben Zhu,Jian-Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种离线强化学习框架，通过传感器位置条件化架构和Point Attention层实现数据驱动的流场控制策略提取，在K-S方程和N-S翼型控制中验证了灵活性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线强化学习方法进行主动流场控制时，需要大量实时高保真环境交互，且传感器配置变化时需要重新训练策略，导致计算成本过高，难以应用于实际工程场景。

Method: 提出离线RL框架，采用传感器位置条件化架构（Position-Conditioned Architecture），通过Point Attention层对传感器位置的空间关系进行建模，使单一策略网络能够自适应多种传感器布局，从离线数据集中提取控制策略。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的混沌抑制和Navier-Stokes方程的翼型流场控制两个代表性问题上验证了方法有效性，表明从数据集中提取策略能为传感器布局优化提供前所未有的灵活性。

Conclusion: 该离线强化学习框架克服了在线方法的计算瓶颈，是实现自适应智能流场控制系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Active flow control is a fundamental application in engineering. Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning have made progress in this field. However, the classical online RL approaches require extensive real-time interactions with the high fidelity environment, while each sensor configuration change necessitates whole policy retraining. All these factors result in prohibitive computational costs for real-world applications. In this work, we propose a novel offline RL framework that addresses both challenges through data-driven policy extraction. We develop a sensor position-conditioned architecture that enables a single policy network to adapt seamlessly to multiple sensor arrangements. The position-conditioned approach incorporated spatial relationship modeling through Point Attention layers to ensure the generalizability to varying sensor placements. We demonstrate the framework on two representative problems, mitigating chaoticity in the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation and flow control over airfoils governed by the Navier-Stokes equation. The result demonstrates that the policy extraction from the dataset provides unprecedented flexibility for sensor placement optimization. This approach represents a significant step towards adaptive, intelligent flow control systems.

</details>


### [30] [OTCache: Optimal Transport for Geometry-Aware Caching in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31026)
*Huanlin Gao,Fang Zhao,Qiang Hui,Fuyuan Shi,Shaoan Zhao,Yantao Li,Chao Tan,Ting Lu,Yuren You,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OTCache提出了一种基于最优传输理论的训练无关缓存调度预测框架，通过参考调度获取、极端低预算下的锚点搜索和目标预算下的分位数插值三阶段流程，在FLUX.1、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上分别实现4.5x、4.7x和3.66x加速，且生成保真度优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的缓存方法通过优化最短路径目标来减少冗余计算，但其加法独立性假设在低NFE（函数评估次数）场景下经常失效，导致缓存调度效果不佳。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在保守预算下使用基于图的缓存方法获得高保真参考调度；(2) 在极端低预算下通过Optuna优化和端到端感知目标进行轻量级锚点搜索；(3) 利用连续扭曲表示在参考策略和锚点策略之间进行分位数插值，预测目标预算下的调度。

Result: 在FLUX.1 [dev]、Qwen-Image和HunyuanVideo上分别实现4.5x、4.7x和3.66x加速，生成保真度持续优于最先进的缓存基线方法。

Conclusion: OTCache通过最优传输启发的调度建模，为加速扩散模型提供了新视角，是一种无需训练且高效的加速框架。

Abstract: We propose OTCache, a training-free framework for accelerating diffusion sampling via caching schedule prediction. Existing graph-based caching methods reduce redundant computation by optimizing shortest-path objectives, but rely on an additive independence assumption, which often breaks down in the low NFE regime. To address this issue, OTCache models caching schedules across inference budgets as a smooth evolution in policy space, inspired by Optimal Transport (OT). The framework consists of three stages: (1) obtaining a high-fidelity \textbf{reference schedule} using a graph-based caching method under a conservative budget; (2) performing a lightweight anchor search under an extreme low-budget setting via Optuna optimization with an end-to-end perceptual objective; and (3) predicting schedules for target budgets via quantile interpolation between the reference and anchor policies using continuous warping representations. Experiments on FLUX.1 [dev], Qwen-Image, and HunyuanVideo show that OTCache achieves 4.5x, 4.7x, and 3.66x acceleration, respectively, while consistently improving generation fidelity over state-of-the-art caching baselines. This work provides a new perspective on accelerating diffusion models through Optimal-Transport-inspired schedule modeling. Code:https://github.com/UnicomAI/OTCache

</details>


### [31] [Teaching LLMs to Recommend and Defer in Underrepresented Epilepsy Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31036)
*Shreyas Rajesh,Kartik Sharma,Tonmoy Monsoor,Mehmet Yigit Turali,Richard Idro,Juliana Kayaga,Robert Sebunya,Tracy Tushabe Namata,Jessica Nichole Pasqua,Vwani Roychowdhury,Rajarshi Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MANANA，一个非参数化提示学习框架，用于在资源受限的乌干达儿科癫痫护理中从纵向非结构化临床笔记预测抗癫痫药物方案。该方法通过将观察到的处方错误转化为可审计的提示记忆，并引入贝叶斯提示平均来生成不确定性感知的延迟信号，在独立验证队列上将访问级top-3处方准确率提高了4-8个百分点，并能在95%精确度下自动处理最自信的一半病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境中，专家癫痫专业知识稀缺，使得基于LLM的决策支持系统对前线临床医生管理长期治疗具有吸引力。然而，这类系统必须适应当地处方实践并知道何时应延迟决策。

Method: 提出MANANA非参数化提示学习框架，从少量患者级训练集中学习本地处方指南，将观察到的处方错误转换为可审计的提示记忆（单智能体和多智能体变体）；提出贝叶斯提示平均，将学习到的提示轨迹转化为处方似然和基于不确定性的延迟信号。

Result: 在独立收集的保留队列上，访问级top-3处方准确率比提示优化基线提高4-8个百分点；选择性预测可实现：系统在最自信的一半病例中达到95%精确度，在最自信的四分之一病例中达到99%精确度，同时将低置信度病例延迟给专家审查。

Conclusion: MANANA框架通过非参数化提示学习和不确定性感知的延迟机制，有效解决了LLM在资源受限环境下进行本地化处方决策的挑战，优于经典ML模型、直接LLM提示和提示优化基线。

Abstract: Specialist epilepsy expertise is scarce in resource-constrained settings, making LLM-based decision support attractive for frontline clinicians managing longitudinal treatment. Such systems must adapt to local prescribing practice and know when to defer. We study this problem in Ugandan pediatric epilepsy care, predicting anti-seizure medication regimens from longitudinal unstructured clinic notes. Standard prompting achieves non-trivial agreement with physician prescriptions, but neurologist review shows that many errors reflect distribution-miscalibrated prescribing defaults rather than failures to parse the local record. We introduce MANANA, a non-parametric prompt-learning framework that learns local prescribing guidance from a small patient-level training set. MANANA converts observed prescription errors into auditable prompt memories, instantiated in single-agent and multi-agent variants, and improves over classical ML models, direct LLM prompting, and prompt-optimization baselines across two independently collected Ugandan cohorts. We further propose Bayesian prompt averaging, which converts the learned prompt trajectory into prescription likelihoods and an uncertainty-based deferral signal. On the independently collected held-out cohort, this improves visit-level top-3 prescription accuracy by 4-8 percentage points over prompt-optimization baselines and enables selective prediction: the system can auto-handle the most confident half of cases at 95% precision, or the most confident quarter at 99% precision, while deferring lower-confidence cases for specialist review.

</details>


### [32] [Warp RL: Reshaping Base Policy Distributions for Dynamics Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31043)
*Ethan Hirschowitz,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Warp RL方法，用可逆的状态条件变换替代传统残差强化学习中的加法修正，从而能重塑基础策略的动作分布（包括方差、置信度、几何形状），在动力学偏移下显著优于加法残差方法，并在仿真和真实机器人任务中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统残差强化学习通过加法修正来适应预训练策略，但这种加法修正只能平移动作分布，无法改变分布的方差、置信度或状态依赖的几何形状。在动力学偏移下，当基础分布与偏移后的系统几何不匹配时，残差修正甚至可能不如未适应的策略，因此需要一种能更灵活调整动作分布结构的适应方法。

Method: Warp RL使用单调有理二次样条流（monotonic rational-quadratic spline flows）构建一个可逆的、状态条件化的变换，对基础策略的动作分布进行"扭曲"（warp）而非加法修正。该方法保持恒等初始化，严格推广了加法残差修正，并提供了一个结构化的适应空间，可同时适用于策略梯度和无梯度优化。

Result: 在ManiSkill3多种操控任务和受控动力学偏移下，Warp RL在仅需平移时与残差修正性能相当，在需要分布重塑时显著超越残差修正。在真实机器人插销插入任务中，Warp RL替代加法残差用于离策略sim-to-real流程，达到相同成功率的同时任务完成速度提升30%。

Conclusion: 加法残差修正的能力本质上局限于分布平移，而Warp RL通过可逆变换提供了更灵活的分布适应能力，在动力学偏移场景下是一种更有效的策略适应方法。

Abstract: Residual reinforcement learning adapts a pretrained robot policy by learning an additive correction to its actions. While effective when adaptation amounts to shifting the base policy's action distribution, additive corrections cannot change the distribution's shape, scale, or state-dependent geometry -- limitations we formalize as wrong variance, miscalibrated confidence, and non-uniform correction. We show that these matter under dynamics shift: when the base distribution is geometrically mismatched to the shifted system, residual correction can underperform even the unadapted policy. We propose \textbf{Warp RL}, a policy adaptation method that replaces additive residuals with an invertible, state-conditioned transformation of the base policy's action distribution. Instantiated with monotonic rational-quadratic spline flows [arXiv:0706.1234v1], Warp RL preserves identity initialization, strictly generalizes additive residual correction, and exposes a structured adaptation space suitable for both policy-gradient and gradient-free optimization. Across a variety of ManiSkill3 manipulation tasks with controlled dynamics shifts, Warp RL matches residual correction when translation is sufficient and substantially outperforms it when adaptation requires distributional reshaping. We further demonstrate that warping can replace additive correction in an off-policy sim-to-real pipeline, achieving comparable success rate with 30% faster task completion on a real-robot peg-insertion task.

</details>


### [33] [Knowledge Distillation from Large Reasoning Models to Compact Student Models: A Case Study on the John O Bryan Mathematics Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31048)
*Gaurab Baral,Aaditya Khanal,Yangyang Tao,Junxiu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过双智能体框架从DeepSeek-R1大推理模型中蒸馏思维链知识到Qwen2.5-7B学生模型，在数学竞赛数据集上实现了4.76个百分点的准确率提升（从64.67%到69.43%），并发现响应长度是影响数学推理质量的关键因素——准确率随推理层级从R1的69.43%（平均220词）下降到R6的41.9%（平均31.2词）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索从大型推理模型（DeepSeek-R1）向轻量级学生模型（Qwen2.5-7B）进行知识蒸馏的可行性，以在保持推理能力的同时降低计算成本。

Method: 使用双智能体框架构建John O'Bryan数学竞赛（2011-2025）的思维链训练语料，通过LoRA低秩适配和MLX框架在Apple Silicon硬件上微调Qwen2.5-7B学生模型。先进行1000次迭代训练发现过拟合后，执行5次受限200次迭代的独立训练并评估稳定性。

Result: 微调后学生模型在竞赛数据集上平均准确率69.43%（标准差0.17%），较基线模型提升4.76个百分点；在MATH-500基准上达到73.1%（标准差0.18%）。对六个推理层级（R1-R6）的分析显示，准确率从R1的69.43%（220词）持续下降至R6的41.9%（31.2词）。

Conclusion: 思维链蒸馏能有效提升紧凑型学生模型的数学推理能力，响应长度是决定推理质量的关键因素，过短的推理链会显著损害准确率。

Abstract: This paper investigates knowledge distillation from a large reasoning model (DeepSeek-R1) to a compact student model (Qwen2.5-7B). Using historical problems from the John O'Bryan Mathematics Competition at Northern Kentucky University (2011-2025), we build a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training corpus through a dual-agent framework. The dataset is used to fine-tune the student model with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on Apple Silicon hardware using the MLX framework. The base Qwen2.5-7B model achieves 64.67% accuracy on competition problems, while the DeepSeek-R1 teacher achieves 91.40%. An initial 1,000-iteration training run revealed severe overfitting, with validation loss reaching a minimum at iteration 200 before rising steadily. Based on this finding, we ran five independent training runs each limited to 200 iterations with varied random seeds to assess result stability. Across these five runs, the fine-tuned student model achieves a mean accuracy of 69.43% (std dev 0.17%) on the competition dataset, a 4.76 percentage-point improvement over the base model, and generalizes to 73.1% (std dev 0.18%) on the MATH-500 benchmark. We further study how response length affects answer quality across six reasoning levels (R1-R6): accuracy declines consistently from 69.43% at R1 (mean 220 words) to 41.9% at R6 (mean 31.2 words), with the two-person speed section most sensitive to token reduction. These results demonstrate that CoT distillation improves compact student models and that response length is a critical factor in mathematical reasoning quality.

</details>


### [34] [Fora: From Weight-Space to Function-Space Protection in Capability-Preserving Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31092)
*Rui Zhou,Tianci Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FORA方法，通过函数空间投影（基于激活协方差的主方向）而非权重空间投影来保护大模型已有能力，在微调时避免灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调保护方法基于权重代理（参数距离、重要性惩罚、输出匹配、权重奇异方向），但未直接考虑能力所依赖的激活方向。能力更应由激活子空间而非权重奇异几何来刻画。

Method: FORA：从无标签校准输入估计每层输入激活协方差的主方向Q，构造右投影器P_Q=I-QQ^T；配合权重SVD的左投影器P_U，更新量为ΔW = P_U M P_Q + U_2 D_δ V_2^T，包含一个禁止读取能力相关函数方向的高容量分支和一个窄谱通道。可扩展为参数高效适配M→(α/r)BA。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上三个设置（COGS/GSM8K学习同时保持翻译，翻译学习同时保持数学），FORA在能力保持上持续优于权重空间投影和标准正则化，仅在数学保持场景下有微小的新任务权衡。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明优势来自投影到能力导出方向（而非权重导出方向），而非投影本身。代码已开源。

Abstract: Full fine-tuning adapts large language models to new tasks but can erode capabilities they already possess. Existing remedies protect through proxies such as parameter distances, importance penalties, output matching, or dominant singular directions of the weights, but none directly asks which activation directions the preserved capability relies on. We argue that a capability is characterized more faithfully by the activation subspace it induces than by the singular geometry of the weight matrix, and develop function-space protection, instantiated as FORA (Function-space Orthogonal Residual Adaptation). From label-free calibration inputs, FORA estimates, per layer, the principal directions $Q$ of the input-activation covariance and forms a right projector $P_Q = I - QQ^T$. Paired with a left projector $P_U$ from the weight SVD, the update is $ΔW = P_U M P_Q + U_2 D_δ V_2^T$: a high-capacity branch structurally barred from reading capability-relevant function directions, plus a narrow spectral channel for controlled plasticity. The construction extends to parameter-efficient adaptation via $M \to (α/r) BA$. Across three settings on Qwen3-1.7B, including COGS and GSM8K learned while preserving translation and translation learned while preserving math, FORA consistently improves preservation over weight-space projection and standard regularization, with only a small new-task trade-off in the math-preservation setting. A controlled ablation isolating the projection source shows that the advantage comes not from projection itself, but from projecting onto capability-derived rather than weight-derived directions. Code is available at https://github.com/zrui239/FORA.

</details>


### [35] [Explaining Machine Learning and Memorization with Statistical Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31110)
*Robin Theriault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用统计力学工具研究神经网络和机器学习理论，重点探讨对抗攻击和隐式低维学习，通过分析密集联想记忆和受限玻尔兹曼机两类模型来理解数据拟合过程中的学习与记忆机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络和机器学习算法在理论上理解不足，导致难以充分发挥其潜力并克服弱点，例如训练过程中的隐式低维学习结构未被充分利用，以及对抗攻击的理论基础尚不明确。

Method: 使用统计力学的数学工具，研究两类数据拟合模型（密集联想记忆DAM和受限玻尔兹曼机RBM），并探究不同版本模型之间的联系以提升分析效率。

Result: 对不同类型神经网络中学习与记忆程度的差异有了更深入的理论理解，建立了不同模型版本间的有用联系。

Conclusion: 通过统计力学方法增进了对神经网络对抗攻击和隐式低维学习的理论理解，并为后续研究提供了更高效的分析框架。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks (NNs) and machine learning (ML) algorithms are poorly understood from a theoretical perspective, which makes it difficult to fully realize their potential and overcome their weaknesses. For instance, ML algorithms train NN weights by moving them along a low-dimensional subspace of their allowed values, but this implicitly low-dimensional learning structure is not properly exploited to improve training because its nature is not well understood. Moreover, trained NNs are easily confused by pervasive adversarial attacks whose theoretical underpinnings are still unclear. This thesis aims to improve our theoretical understanding of NNs and ML, with a particular focus on adversarial attacks and implicitly low-dimensional learning. For this purpose, we use mathematical tools from statistical mechanics to study different types of NNs and ways in which they can fit the data. In particular, we study two classes of models that fit the data with various degrees of learning and memorization: dense associative memory (DAM) and restricted Boltzmann machines (RBM). In the process, we investigate connections between different versions of these models that are useful to make analytical investigations more efficient.

</details>


### [36] [Visualizing High-Dimensional Graph Embeddings via Informed Multi-View Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31119)
*Ya Ji,Xuefeng Li,Timo Brand,Jacob Miller,Peng Zhang,Stephen Kobourov,Yifan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在高维空间嵌入图结构，并通过可微分代理函数搜索优化美学和可读性指标的最佳二维视角，结合交互系统DataFly揭示传统布局中隐藏的结构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二维图可视化在解释空间关系方面有效，但常常扭曲高维结构信息，导致部分结构模式在二维布局中被隐藏。

Method: 将图嵌入高维空间，通过可微分的边交叉代理函数搜索最优二维视角，优化美学和可读性指标（如边交叉数、角分辨率）。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在优化指标上持续优于标准二维布局，甚至超过专门优化这些指标的现有方法；用户研究证明能揭示传统二维可视化中隐藏的结构模式。

Conclusion: 高维嵌入结合可微分视角搜索能有效改善图可视化的质量，交互系统DataFly有助于探索多视角下的图结构。

Abstract: Graphs are commonly visualized in 2D, where humans readily interpret spatial relationships, yet such layouts often distort higher-dimensional structure. We propose to embed graphs in high-dimensional space and search for informative 2D viewpoints that optimize aesthetic and readability metrics (e.g., edge crossings and angular resolution), enabled by a novel differentiable surrogate for edge crossings. Numerical experiments show that these viewpoints consistently outperform standard 2D layouts, and can even surpass methods explicitly designed to optimize these metrics. We further introduce DataFly, an interactive system for exploring multiple candidate viewpoints through seamless navigation. A usability study demonstrates that our approach reveals structural patterns that remain hidden in conventional 2D visualizations.

</details>


### [37] [A Bayesian Filtering Approach for Learning Lagrangian Dynamics from Noisy Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31137)
*Kundan Kumar,Shreya Das,Simo Särkkä*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯滤波的方法，利用拉格朗日力学和神经网络参数化，从含噪部分观测中联合学习物理系统的动力学参数和状态，并通过最大似然和高斯近似贝叶斯滤波器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拉格朗日神经网络（LNN）只能从完整、无噪声的观测中学习，无法处理部分、含噪的实测数据。本文旨在将拉格朗日力学框架与贝叶斯滤波相结合，以应对真实物理系统中的噪声和不完全观测问题。

Method: 1) 将系统动力学建模为拉格朗日力学形式，用神经网络参数化动能和势能；2) 将未知外力建模为高斯白噪声，从而得到连续时间随机状态空间模型（SSM）；3) 通过最大似然方法联合学习神经网络参数和系统状态，使用基于高斯近似的贝叶斯滤波器（如扩展卡尔曼滤波或无迹卡尔曼滤波）进行推断。

Result: 在摆锤和杜芬振荡器两个基准系统上验证了方法有效性，并与传统LNN以及使用已知系统模型的近似贝叶斯滤波器进行了性能对比，结果表明所提方法在含噪部分观测下具有优势。

Conclusion: 将拉格朗日神经网络与贝叶斯滤波结合，能够在部分、含噪观测条件下有效学习物理系统的动力学，为数据驱动的物理建模提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Bayesian filtering-based approach for learning the dynamics of a physical system from partial, noisy measurements. We model the system dynamics using a Lagrangian mechanics formulation. As in Lagrangian neural networks (LNNs), we parameterize the kinetic and potential energies with neural networks. The unknown external forces in the Lagrangian formulation are modeled as white Gaussian noise. The corresponding Euler--Lagrange equations then yield a continuous-time stochastic state-space model (SSM) that describes the system dynamics. The neural network parameters and system states are then jointly learned via a maximum-likelihood method using Gaussian-approximation-based Bayesian filters. The effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated on pendulum and Duffing oscillator examples, and its performance is compared with conventional LNNs and with approximate Bayesian filters using known system models.

</details>


### [38] [PPT-Eval: A Benchmark for Computer-Use Agents on PowerPoint Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31154)
*Apurva Gandhi,Vishwas Suryanarayanan,Raja Hasnain Anwar,Firoz Shaik,Shubhang Desai,Thong Q. Nguyen,Muhammad Taqi Raza,Vishal Chowdhary,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PPT-Eval，一个包含120个PowerPoint任务的基准测试，并设计了基于评分标准的评估框架以提供部分信用评分和自然语言反馈。实验表明，当前最先进的AI代理（如Claude-4.5-Opus）仅能达到45%的成功率和57%的平均部分得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻灯片创建与编辑是多模态活动，在专业和教育场景中广泛存在，是测试现实世界计算机使用代理的理想场景。然而，现有评估方法缺乏对复杂多模态任务的细粒度评价，二元成功指标无法捕捉代理的部分进展。

Method: 构建了包含12个文件、120个PowerPoint任务的基准测试，覆盖内容创建和演示编辑场景并按难度分级。设计了一个基于评分标准的评估框架，为任务创建特定评分标准，给予中间步骤部分信用，惩罚不必要更改和不良美学，并提供自然语言反馈。

Result: 所提出的评估框架与人类判断的Kendall's τ-b相关系数达到0.77。现有前沿代理在PowerPoint任务上表现不佳，Claude-4.5-Opus仅达到45%的成功率和57%的平均部分得分。

Conclusion: 当前的AI代理在处理复杂的PowerPoint任务方面仍有显著不足，本文提出的细粒度评估方法能更有效地衡量代理的实际能力。

Abstract: Creating and editing slides is a rich, multimodal activity that is ubiquitous in professional and educational settings, making it an ideal testbed for real-world computer-use agents. Microsoft PowerPoint is among the most widely adopted and feature-rich environments for presentation creation. We introduce PPT-Eval, a benchmark of 120 PowerPoint tasks across 12 files that cover both content creation and presentation editing scenarios, organized by difficulty. A central challenge in this domain is evaluation: tasks are complex, multimodal, and often admit many valid solutions. Moreover, today's agents frequently make only partial progress, which binary success metrics fail to capture. To address this, we design a robust evaluation framework to help create task-specific rubrics for PowerPoint tasks, taking inspiration from and building on past works for rubric-based evaluation. These rubrics award partial credit for intermediate steps, penalize unnecessary changes and poor aesthetics, and provide natural language feedback. This nuanced approach proves highly effective, achieving a Kendall's τ-b correlation of 0.77 with human judgments. We find that existing frontier agents still struggle with solving PowerPoint tasks, with strong models like Claude-4.5-Opus achieving only a 45% success rate and an average partial score of 57%. The benchmark is located at: https://microsoft.github.io/ppteval.

</details>


### [39] [ComplianceGate: Classifier-Gated Multi-Tier LLM Routing for Inference in Regulated Industries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31163)
*Abhishek Dey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分类器门控路由架构，通过在推理前对查询进行分类，将包含PII的查询路由到本地端点，将简单查询路由到小型快速模型，在保证合规性的同时实现39%延迟降低和33-52%成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在受监管行业中面临合规执行与成本效率的双重约束，PII数据可能在系统判断前就到达模型端点，且所有查询都通过单一大型模型处理导致GPU资源浪费，缺乏地理路由机制。

Method: 提出分类器门控路由架构：在解码推理之前放置一个经过训练的编码器分类器，评估每个查询的复杂度和数据敏感性，然后将其路由到适当大小、适当地理位置的小型密集模型。包含PII的查询在LLM计算开始前路由到本地端点，简单查询路由到小型快速模型。

Result: 在600个查询上评估：中位数延迟降低39%，成本节省33-52%（取决于查询分布），生成吞吐量122-200 tokens/秒（基线50-64），编码器分类器准确率99.2%，PII召回率接近完美，推理开销仅7ms。

Conclusion: 预推理分类是实现合规性设计的LLM部署的实用路径，通过结构设计使数据驻留违规在架构上不可能发生。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in regulated industries operate under two constraints: compliance enforcement and cost efficiency. Personally identifiable information (PII) in user queries can reach model endpoints before the system determines whether that data should leave its jurisdictional boundary. Serving all queries through a single large model consumes full GPU capacity regardless of query complexity while offering no mechanism for geographic routing. Mixture-of-Experts architectures do not address this routing occurs between expert layers within the model after data has already arrived at the endpoint, with all experts loaded in memory regardless of query complexity. We propose a classifier-gated routing architecture that enforces compliance by design. A trained encoder classifier sits before any decoder inference, evaluating each query for complexity and data sensitivity, then routing it to an appropriately sized dense model in the appropriate geographic location. PII-containing queries route to local endpoints before any LLM computation begins, making data residency violations structurally impossible. Simple queries reach small, fast models at a fraction of the cost. Our evaluation on 600 queries demonstrates 39% median latency reduction, 33-52% cost savings depending on query distribution, and generation throughput of 122-200 tokens/second versus 50-64 for the baseline. The encoder classifier achieves 99.2% accuracy with near-perfect PII recall at 7ms inference overhead, establishing pre-inference classification as a practical path to compliance-by-design LLM deployment.

</details>


### [40] [AETDICE: Unified Framework and Offline Optimization for Nonlinear Multi-Objective RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31178)
*Woosung Kim,Youngjun Suh,Jinho Lee,Jongmin Lee,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AET框架统一了非线性多目标强化学习中的标量化期望回报（SER）和期望标量化回报（ESR）两种范式，并基于该框架开发了AETDICE算法，利用DICE风格密度比估计在增广状态空间中进行样本优化，解决了长期存在的优化策略碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性多目标强化学习中的SER和ESR两种范式导致优化策略碎片化，缺乏统一的数学框架来桥接两者，限制了非线性偏好优化的理论发展和实际应用。

Method: 提出AET（聚合-期望-变换）三元分解框架，将标量化过程分解为聚合、期望、变换三个步骤，统一SER和ESR；在此基础上设计AETDICE算法，利用DICE密度比估计在增广状态空间中进行离线样本优化。

Result: AETDICE能够在静态数据集上实现基于样本的优化，解决了现有方法无法处理的AET框架下的权衡问题。

Conclusion: AET框架为通用非线性MORL提供了原理性基础，AETDICE作为该框架下的可解离线算法，有效弥合了SER与ESR之间的鸿沟，推动了非线性多目标强化学习的发展。

Abstract: Optimizing nonlinear preferences in multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) is essential for capturing complex trade-offs like risk aversion or fairness. However, such non-linearity has historically bifurcated nonlinear MORL objectives into two distinct paradigms: Scalarized Expected Return (SER) and Expected Scalarized Return (ESR). While SER requires global-level optimization and ESR requires non-Markovian policies, leading to fragmented optimization strategies, we bridge this divide through the Aggregation-Expectation-Transformation (AET) framework. By unifying both criteria through a tripartite decomposition of scalarization, AET provides a principled foundation for general nonlinear MORL. Building on this framework, we propose AETDICE, a tractable offline RL algorithm for AET objectives. By utilizing DICE-style density-ratio estimation in an augmented state space, AETDICE enables sample-based optimization from static datasets. Our framework resolves long-standing barriers and captures respective trade-offs induced by AET framework, which existing methods fail to address.

</details>

*Jiachun Li,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯上下文实验者：通过Transformer策略模仿贝叶斯后验Neyman教师，实现自适应实验的方差估计与分配过程的摊销，从而高效估计平均处理效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应实验中，最优分配（Neyman规则）依赖于未知的潜在结果方差，需进行序贯方差估计与分配。现有方法计算成本高且难以泛化，缺乏可摊销的学习框架。

Method: 引入贝叶斯上下文实验者：1）贝叶斯后验Neyman教师：基于非参数贝叶斯信念更新，计算后验Neyman处理概率；2）Transformer学生策略：通过注意力机制计算充分统计量，结合投影梯度下降模仿教师更新；3）针对未知光滑性，提出混合专家Transformer，门控机制作为光滑类层次后验，聚焦近最优专家。

Result: 实验表明Transformer能够准确模仿教师策略，实现自适应分配，相比基线方法显著提升ATE估计精度。理论证明可通过经验风险最小化在监督预训练下学习该摊销策略。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将自适应实验的序贯最优分配问题纳入上下文学习框架，为因果推断中的实验设计提供了可学习、可泛化的摊销解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive experiments for average treatment effects (ATE) require randomized allocations balancing valid inference with statistical efficiency. The oracle design is a covariate-dependent Neyman rule governed by unknown arm-conditional outcome variances. We investigate whether this sequential variance-estimation and allocation process can be amortized via in-context learning. We introduce Bayesian in-context experimenters: transformer policies trained to imitate a Bayesian posterior Neyman teacher. The teacher updates nonparametric beliefs over potential outcomes using experimental history to assign posterior Neyman treatment probabilities. This design converges to the oracle rule, supporting efficient ATE inference. Transformers constructively implement this mapping through attention-based sufficient statistics and projected gradient descent, imitating Bayesian updating for Gaussian-series priors. To address unknown outcome smoothness, we combine smoothness-indexed experimenters using a mixture-of-experts transformer. The gate acts as a hierarchical posterior over smoothness classes, concentrating on near-oracle experts. By bounding the complexity of the transformer class, we prove this amortized policy can be learned via empirical risk minimization using supervised pretraining. Experiments confirm accurate teacher imitation, adaptive allocation, and improved ATE precision over baselines.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决冻结LLM在严格情节重置的持续学习条件下，由于无法更新参数而难以从过往经验中积累和泛化数学推理策略的问题。
