We present FARS (Fully Automated Research System), a fully automated AI-for-AI research system designed to operate across research topics at scale. FARS autonomously generates and advances projects through ideation, planning, experimentation, and writing, using stage-specific agents coordinated through a shared workspace that records proposals, code, logs, results, and manuscripts. In its first public deployment, FARS produced 166 complete research papers spanning 67 fine-grained AI/ML topics while preserving intermediate artifacts as an auditable corpus rather than a curated set of successes. We evaluate this corpus with 282 structured reviews from volunteer reviewers covering 140 papers, including overall ratings, sub-scores, integrity checks, and LLM-use disclosure. The reviews indicate that FARS can produce review-worthy and occasionally strong AI/ML research artifacts in a large-scale public deployment, while also exposing recurring failure modes in narrow experimental scope, methodological limitations, and integrity issues.

</details>


### [150] [Arena-T2I Hard: Benchmarking and Improving Faithfulness with Dependency-Aware Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31711)
*Yuanhao Ban,Tong Xie,Sohyun An,Yunqi Hong,Evan Frick,I-Hung Hsu,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Arena-T2I Hard，一个包含310个压力测试提示的文本到图像生成忠实度基准。每个提示分解为约30个是/否约束（共六类），发现最强闭源系统得分仅0.855，且竞技场排名无法预测忠实度。此外，提出依赖感知检查表奖励（DAG结构）与组解耦归一化（GDPO）结合的方法，在忠实度-美学权衡上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有忠实度基准依赖简单原子指令，顶级模型已近满分；当T2I模型用于创意工作流时，用户提出多层面复杂请求（空间关系、风格约束、文本渲染等），单一二值VLM评分无法捕获模型在哪些具体约束上失败。

Method: 1) 构建 Arena-T2I Hard 基准：从真实竞技场日志中提取310个复杂提示，每个分解为约30个是/否约束（覆盖六类：空间关系、风格、文本渲染等）；2) 提出依赖感知检查表奖励：将提示分解为有向无环图（DAG）的是/否问题，父约束失败则零化子约束；3) 组解耦归一化（GDPO）：将BT美学奖励与忠实度奖励进行标准化，使两者在各自生成组内互不坍缩。

Result: 最强闭源系统得分仅0.855，11个系统间存在33个百分点的性能差距；竞技场高排名与忠实度不相关（BT偏好分数优先美学而非精细提示遵循）；所提方法在SD3.5-Medium和FLUX.1-dev上获得严格更好的忠实度-美学权衡，超越单奖励、加权和及4奖励BT集成基线。

Conclusion: 需要专门针对多约束复杂提示的忠实度评估基准；依赖感知检查表奖励将忠实度转化为可训练的逐约束信号，GDPO实现忠实度与美学之间更好的权衡，两者结合是当前最优方案。

Abstract: Faithfulness -- how precisely a generated image aligns with its prompt -- is increasingly central to the real-world utility of text-to-image (T2I) models. Existing faithfulness benchmarks, however, rely on simple atomic instructions, on which top-tier systems already achieve near-perfect scores. As T2I models enter creative workflows, users issue multi-faceted requests combining intricate spatial relationships, stylistic constraints, and complex text rendering. In this setting, a single binary VLM-judge score no longer captures which specific constraints the model fails to satisfy. We introduce Arena-T2I Hard, a 310-prompt stress benchmark drawn from real arena T2I logs, with approximately 30 decomposed yes/no constraints per prompt spanning six categories, including text rendering. The strongest closed-source system we evaluate reaches 0.855 with a 33~pp performance gap across 11 systems, demonstrating substantial discriminative power. Moreover, high public-arena rankings fail to predict faithfulness, confirming that holistic Bradley-Terry (BT) preference scores prioritize aesthetics over fine-grained prompt adherence. We propose a dependency-aware checklist reward that decomposes each prompt into a DAG of yes/no questions and zeroes descendants of failed parents, turning faithfulness into a per-constraint training signal. Combined with a BT aesthetic reward via group-decoupled normalization (GDPO), which standardizes each reward within its rollout group so neither collapses, the recipe attains a strictly better faithfulness-aesthetics trade-off on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev under MMRB2 pairwise comparisons than every single-reward, naive weighted-sum, or 4-reward BT-ensemble baseline.

</details>


### [151] [A Self-Evolving Agentic System for Automated Generation and Execution of Biological Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31763)
*Yankai Jiang,Weiting Tang,Haoran Sun,Zhenyu Tang,Yuejie Hou,Yingnan Han,Rubo Wang,Yueyuxiao Yang,Cheng Liang,Lilong Wang,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Lei Bai,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtoPilot是一个自演化多智能体系统，能够将生物实验方案（SOP）自动转换为可执行的SDK兼容代码，并基于湿实验反馈进行迭代修正。在包含294个合成生物学和分子生物学任务的基准测试中，其协议到代码的门控通过率达到89.5%，Opentrons执行通过率88.24%，远高于OpenTrons-AI的32.35%，湿实验验证也成功获得了Sanger测序确认的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主湿实验自动化需要将生物意图、定量流程、设备约束和实验反馈从协议设计到代码执行全链路对齐，而现有方法仅能生成看似合理的协议文本，缺乏对执行可行性的保障。

Method: ProtoPilot采用自演化多智能体系统架构，包含层级可验证性、多智能体编排和运行时更新的技能库。它逐步将协议扩展为SOP、合成SDK兼容代码，并根据湿实验反馈修正工作流。同时构建了包含294个合成生物学和分子生物学任务的专家基准评估框架，涵盖专家评分标准、设备级有效性门控和真实实验测试。

Result: Top@3专家偏好率90.2%，协议到代码门控通过率89.5%，Opentrons通过率88.24%（对比OpenTrons-AI的32.35%），湿实验验证产出可解释读数、Sanger确认产物及反馈修正的PCA组装DNA靶标。

Conclusion: 该评估框架能够捕获自主湿实验自动化的执行相关需求，ProtoPilot通过将协议和代码生成转化为已验证的执行和反馈引导的修正，能够满足这些需求，为自主实验建立了可验证的路径。

Abstract: Autonomous wet-lab experimentation requires more than plausible protocol text: biological intent, quantitative procedures, device constraints and experimental feedback must remain aligned from protocol and SOP design to code and physical execution. We developed ProtoPilot, a self-evolving multi-agent system, together with an expert-grounded benchmark and evaluation framework for testing this conversion as an experimental automation problem. The framework spans 294 synthetic-biology and molecular-biology tasks derived from 98 gold-standard protocols, wet-lab expert rubrics, device-level validity gates and real experimental tests. ProtoPilot incorporates layer-wise verifiability, multi-agent orchestration and a runtime-updated skill library to generate protocols, expand SOPs, synthesize SDK-compliant code and revise workflows from wet-lab feedback. It achieved a Top@3 expert-preference rate of 90.2%, an overall protocol-to-code gate pass rate of 89.5% and an Opentrons pass rate of 88.24%, compared with 32.35% for OpenTrons-AI. Wet-lab validation produced interpretable readouts, Sanger-confirmed products and feedback-corrected PCA-assembled DNA targets, establishing a verifiable route to autonomous experimentation. Together, these results show that the evaluation framework captures execution-relevant requirements for autonomous wet-lab automation, and that ProtoPilot can meet them by converting protocol and code generation into validated execution and feedback-guided revision.

</details>


### [152] [Evo-PI: Aligning Medical Reasoning via Evolving Principle-Guided Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31800)
*Xianda Zheng,Huan Gao,Meng-Fen Chiang,Michael Witbrock,Kaiqi Zhao,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Evo-PI，一种将推理原则作为可演化的语言监督信号的框架，通过原则与模型推理能力的协同进化来动态调整监督信号，在医疗视觉问答等复杂推理任务上显著提升准确率（最高提升24.6%），为多模态大模型的推理训练提供了可扩展的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs的推理能力受限于静态监督（固定提示、规则或奖励模型），这些信号只能约束输出格式，无法塑造底层的推理过程，导致在复杂决策任务中泛化能力弱、性能易饱和。

Method: Evo-PI将推理原则作为显式的、基于语言的监督信号，构建原则与模型的协同进化循环：原则指导模型推理，模型行为反过来优化原则。通过这种动态对齐机制，监督信号能够逐步适应模型的推理缺陷。

Result: 在医疗视觉问答的8个基准测试和多个模型骨干上，Evo-PI一致地提升了推理准确性，最高提升达24.6%。

Conclusion: 演化式原则引导的监督为训练专家级对齐推理能力提供了一种可扩展且通用的范式。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, the reasoning capabilities of large multimodal language models (MLLMs) remain fundamentally constrained by static supervision, where fixed prompts, rules, or reward models provide non-adaptive guidance throughout training. Such static signals are often sufficient to enforce output formats, but fail to shape the underlying reasoning process, leading to brittle generalization and performance saturation in complex decision-making tasks. We propose Evo-PI, a principle-centric learning framework that treats reasoning principles as explicit, language-based supervision signals that can be generated, evaluated, and iteratively evolved. Instead of relying on fixed rewards, Evo-PI enables a co-evolutionary loop in which principles guide model reasoning, while model behaviors in turn refine the principles that supervise them. This dynamic alignment mechanism allows supervision to progressively adapt to the model's reasoning deficiencies. We instantiate Evo-PI in medical visual question answering as a high-stakes testbed requiring structured visual-textual reasoning. Across eight benchmarks and multiple model backbones, Evo-PI consistently improves reasoning accuracy, achieving gains of up to 24.6%. Our results suggest that evolving principle-guided supervision offers a scalable and general paradigm for training expert-aligned reasoning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/zhengxianda/Evo_PI.

</details>


### [153] [RAISE: LLM-based Automated Heuristic Design with Robust Adversary Instance Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31801)
*Fei Liu,Alessio Figalli,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAISE框架，将约束对抗实例搜索集成到LLM进化搜索循环中，解决LLM自动启发式设计在分布偏移下的鲁棒性问题。实验表明，现有方法在分布偏移下性能下降最高19倍，RAISE在所有测试分布和问题规模上保持稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based AHD方法在固定训练实例集上优化启发式，但在实际部署中面临分布偏移时会灾难性失败，缺乏鲁棒性保障。

Method: RAISE将鲁棒AHD建模为约束对抗实例搜索问题：外层循环通过LLM算子进化启发式，内层循环（无LLM）在训练实例集的epsilon邻域内高效搜索困难实例，采用基分布参数化与边界投影。

Result: 在在线装箱、在线作业车间调度、在线车辆路径规划三个问题及五个分布族上，现有LLM-AHD方法在分布偏移下性能下降最高19倍，RAISE在所有分布和问题规模上持续保持强性能。

Conclusion: RAISE通过约束对抗搜索有效提升了LLM自动启发式设计对分布偏移的鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Automated Heuristic Design (AHD) with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown remarkable progress in discovering high-quality heuristics. However, existing LLM-based AHD methods optimize heuristics for a fixed training instance set and may fail catastrophically when deployed under real-world distributional shifts. We propose Robust Adversary Instance Search (RAISE), a framework that integrates constrained worst-case instance search within a principled neighborhood of the training distribution into the LLM-based evolutionary search loop. RAISE treats robust AHD as a constrained adversarial instance search problem: the outer loop evolves heuristics via LLM operators, while an LLM-free inner loop efficiently identifies hard instances within an epsilon-ball around the training instance set using a basis distribution parameterization with boundary projection. Comprehensive experiments on Online Bin Packing (OBP), Online Job Shop Scheduling (OJSP), and Online Vehicle Routing (OVRP) across five distribution families demonstrate that existing LLM-based AHD methods degrade by up to 19 times under distribution shift, while RAISE consistently maintains strong performance across all tested distributions and problem scales

</details>


### [154] [Large Databases Need Small, Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31808)
*Parker Glenn,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明，在仅16GB显存的本地环境下运行的量化开源模型，在LM-数据库集成任务中能够匹配或超越闭源API模型的准确率，同时成本降低390倍、延迟降低3.8倍，挑战了闭源LM API不可或缺的普遍假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于专有API的语言模型系统在大型数据库场景下采用token计费模式，单次实验成本可超过1万美元，严重阻碍了深入研究与实际部署。为解决这一成本瓶颈，需要探索更经济的替代方案。

Method: 使用量化开源模型在本地16GB显存环境下运行，将其集成到BlendSQL v0.1.0框架中，并分析高效部署这些开源模型所需的关键系统优化。

Result: 相比专有LM API，本地量化开源模型实现了390倍的成本降低和3.8倍的延迟降低，同时在准确率上与闭源模型相当或更优。

Conclusion: 闭源LM API并非LM-数据库集成的必要条件，量化开源模型在本地部署是一种成本更低、延迟更短且性能不妥协的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Language model systems built around proprietary APIs often operate on a token-based cost model. This becomes prohibitively expensive in the context of large databases, where LM-enhanced relational operators can incur costs exceeding $10,000 for a single set of experiments, hindering thorough research and practical deployment. In this paper, we demonstrate that quantized, open-weight models running locally on just 16GB of VRAM can match or exceed the accuracy of closed-source counterparts at lower latency and a fraction of the price, challenging the prevailing assumption that closed-source LM APIs are necessary for effective LM-database integration. We present and analyze the key system optimizations required to efficiently deploy these open-weight models within an LM-DB system. By integrating these local models into the BlendSQL v0.1.0 framework, we demonstrate a 390x reduction in overall costs and 3.8x reduction in latency compared to a proprietary LM API. We make our code available at https://github.com/CapitalOne-Research/play-by-the-type-rules/tree/main/sembench.

</details>


### [155] [Creating Intelligence: A Computational Foundation for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31819)
*Peter Overmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于集合论和超维度计算的全新认知计算理论，使用稀疏二进制数据和拓扑可塑性替代传统神经网络的连续权重与矩阵运算，通过子集模式匹配实现联想记忆，并映射到小脑和新皮层的神经解剖结构，为高能效合成智能开辟新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络依赖连续权重和矩阵乘法，与生物神经系统的离散脉冲编码机制存在根本性差异，作者希望建立一种更贴近生物神经群体编码的计算理论，避免连续瓶颈。

Method: 采用稀疏二进制数据表示信息为离散集合；利用组合扩展隐藏层的网络拓扑自然涌现联想记忆；学习机制为拓扑可塑性而非标量权重调整；核心算法是子集模式匹配和精确最近邻搜索，操作复杂度为常数时间。

Result: 该架构统一了自联想和异联想学习；实现了感知数据（稀疏分布表示）与符号（稀疏全息表示）之间的桥接；算法可直接翻译为存内硬件实现。

Conclusion: 子集模式匹配是认知的基本引擎；小脑和新皮层均实现该算法的变体；基于离散逻辑而非矩阵算术的路线有望实现人类水平能效的合成智能。

Abstract: This work introduces a new computational theory of mind grounded in set theory and hyperdimensional computing. Whereas traditional neural networks rely on continuous weights and matrix multiplication, this framework works with sparse binary data. It represents information as discrete sets, directly modeling biological neural population codes. I demonstrate that associative memory emerges naturally from network topologies featuring a combinatorially expanded hidden layer. Learning is driven by topological plasticity rather than scalar weight adjustments. This architecture unifies auto-associative and hetero-associative learning under a single core algorithm: information retrieval via subset pattern matching and exact nearest-neighbor search. Operating with constant-time complexity, these mechanisms bridge perceptual data (sparse distributed representations) and symbols (sparse holographic representations) without continuous bottlenecks. Mapping this framework to neuroanatomy, I propose that both the cerebellum and the neocortex implement variants of this algorithm, making subset pattern matching the fundamental engine of cognition. Because it relies on discrete logic rather than matrix arithmetic, this algorithm translates directly into in-memory hardware. This opens a new route toward synthetic intelligence with human-level energy efficiency.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Cluster-First Route-Second Decomposition for Industrial-Scale Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31820)
*Oguzhan Karaahmetoglu,Hyong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的自适应先聚类后路径（CFRS）方法，将CVRP分解建模为迭代决策过程。LLM作为高层决策者，根据分解状态动态选择聚类、平衡和优化算子，联合划分客户与车辆，实现容量感知聚类。在多达50万客户的合成与基准实例上评估，该方法在基准规模实例上表现竞争力强，在更大规模问题上展现出更好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFRS方法依赖固定划分规则、预定义优化目标或学习策略，在不同空间、需求与操作特征的实例上表现不一致，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 提出自适应CFRS系统，将分解过程形式化为迭代决策；以LLM作为高层决策者，分析演化中的分解状态，选择性应用聚类、平衡和精细化算子；联合划分客户与车辆，实现容量感知且适应问题特征的聚类。

Result: 在合成和基准CVRP实例（多达50万客户）上评估，基准规模实例上表现竞争力强，在更大规模问题上可扩展性和鲁棒性更佳。

Conclusion: LLM引导的自适应决策支持可作为工业规模车辆路径和大规模物流规划的实用方法。

Abstract: Large-scale capacitated vehicle routing problems (CVRPs) are commonly addressed using cluster-first route-second (CFRS) approaches that split a routing instance into smaller, computationally tractable subproblems. Existing splitting methods typically rely on fixed partitioning rules, predefined optimization objectives, or learned policies, which may perform inconsistently across instances exhibiting different spatial, demand, and operational characteristics. In this work, we propose an adaptive CFRS system that formulates a decomposition procedure as an iterative decision-making process. Motivated by the recent success of large language models (LLMs) in reasoning and tool selection, the system employs an LLM as a high-level decision maker that analyzes the evolving decomposition state and selectively applies further clustering, balancing, and refinement operators. The proposed algorithm jointly partitions customers and vehicles, enabling capacity-aware clustering while adapting partitioning decisions to the characteristics of each problem. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and benchmark-derived CVRP instances containing up to 500,000 customers. Experimental results demonstrate competitive performance on benchmark-scale instances while exhibiting improved scalability and robust routing quality on substantially larger problems. These results highlight the potential of adaptive, LLM-guided decision support as a practical approach for industrial-scale vehicle routing and large-scale logistics planning.

</details>


### [157] [An Agentic AI Framework to Accelerate Scientific Discovery in Plant Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31831)
*Renan Souza,Daniel Rosendo,Kelsey Carter,John Lagergren,Frédéric Suter,Shelaine L. Curd,Gerald A. Tuskan,Rafael Ferreira da Silva,David Weston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端自主AI框架，将高通量植物表型分析从专家手工操作转变为由AI代理驱动的交互式自主发现平台，使原本需要数天至数周的分析过程缩短到秒级交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量植物表型分析设施每天产生海量图像数据，但性状提取和解释仍依赖人工专家且严格事后进行，分析而非数据采集成为科学发现的主要瓶颈。

Method: 构建双代理架构：对话式协同科学家代理将自然语言问题转化为结构化分析计划，无头计算代理在Frontier百亿亿次超级计算机上执行Vision Transformer分割和性状提取；两代理运行在独立安全域，通过令牌认证的安全流通道通信，确保端到端溯源。

Result: 将原本数天至数周的分析流程转变为秒级交互循环，代理可推理结果、推荐后续分析并回应追问。

Conclusion: 该框架将表型设施从数据工厂转变为交互式自主发现平台，科学家与AI代理协同加速科学洞察，同时解决了云原生框架忽略的联邦、数据移动和溯源等现实问题。

Abstract: High-throughput plant phenotyping now generates image derived datasets far faster than scientists can analyze them. At Oak Ridge National Laboratory's Advanced Plant Phenotyping Laboratory (APPL), automated stations image hundreds of plants daily across multiple remote sensing modalities; yet, trait extraction and interpretation remain manual, expert-bound, and strictly post-hoc, making analysis, not acquisition, the binding constraint on discovery. We present an end-to-end agentic AI framework that turns the facility from a data factory into an interactive autonomous, discovery platform, where scientists partner with AI agents to accelerate time to insight. A conversational Co-Scientist Agent translates a scientist's natural-language question into a structured analysis plan, and a headless Compute Agent dispatches Vision Transformer segmentation and trait extraction on the Frontier exascale supercomputer. The two agents run in separate security and resource domains and communicate over a secure, token-authenticated streaming channel, a design that accounts for the federation, data-movement, and provenance realities cloud-native agentic frameworks ignore, ensuring end-to-end provenance is captured for every interaction. The framework turns a days- to weeks-long analysis process into an interactive loop where agents reason over results, recommend next analyses, and respond to follow-up questions in seconds.

</details>


### [158] [Harnessing Textual Refusal Directions for Multimodal Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31876)
*Moreno D'Incà,Massimiliano Mancini,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS方法，利用LLM基座中的文本拒绝方向跨模态泛化到图像/视频，无需多模态安全数据即可实现多模态LLM的安全对齐，在保持实用性的同时提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM的安全对齐要么需要后训练对齐，要么利用激活空间中的拒绝方向，但两者都依赖难以收集的多模态不安全数据。本文放松这一约束，探究文本拒绝方向能否跨模态泛化。

Method: 提出Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering (MARS)方法：通过激活重中心化校正模态错配，在几何定义的信任区域内自适应缩放引导强度，选择最优干预层，仅在第一个生成的token上操作。无需训练，无需多模态安全数据。

Result: 在五个SOTA多模态LLM上的安全、实用性和视频越狱基准测试中，MARS在保持实用性的同时实现了持续的安全提升。

Conclusion: 安全相关的结构在模态间是共享的，文本拒绝方向是多模态对齐中强大且未被充分探索的基础。

Abstract: To improve safety in Large Language Models (LLMs) we can either perform post-training alignment or exploit refusal directions in the activation space. Both strategies are less feasible in Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) as they require unsafe multimodal data, harder to collect than their unimodal counterpart. In this work, we relax this constraint and investigate whether textual refusal directions, extracted directly from the LLM backbone, generalize across modalities (i.e., image, video). Preliminary findings confirm this ability, though effectiveness is conditioned by layer selection, steering strength, and cross-modal alignment, with the latter causing safe multimodal inputs to be spuriously steered toward refusal. Building on this, we introduce Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering (MARS), a light-weight training-free approach that injects multimodal safety without the need for multimodal safety data. MARS corrects modality misalignment via activation re-centering, adaptively scales steering strength within a geometrically defined trust region, and selects the optimal intervention layer, operating at the first generated token. Evaluated on five SOTA MLLMs across safety, utility, and video jailbreak benchmarks, MARS achieves consistent safety gains while preserving utility. These results reveal that safety-relevant structure is shared across modalities and that textual refusal directions are a powerful and underexplored foundation for multimodal alignment.

</details>


### [159] [TreeAgent: A Generalizable Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Bias Labeling in Forestry via Compiled Expert Rules and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31976)
*Shiyi Chen,Nicholas Saban,Collin Hargreaves,Huiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体系统（MAS），将专家决策树与视觉语言模型（VLM）结合，通过解耦声明式决策（D³）框架实现零修改泛化，在树木偏斜分类任务中以更低标注成本超越监督学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家标注存在速度慢、不一致性高、可扩展性差等问题，是林业遥感中树高偏斜分类等任务的瓶颈；同时人工标注数据存在标注者间变异，亟需降低专家标注成本并保持可解释性。

Method: 采用多智能体系统（MAS），将专家决策树作为结构先验，由VLM在决策树各节点执行局部语义感知，并通过多智能体投票机制缓解VLM的随机性；提出解耦声明式决策（D³）框架，实现无需修改即可泛化到不同专家定义的决策结构。

Result: 在树木偏斜分类测试中，该方法优于有监督机器学习基线，并显著减少了专家标注工作量。

Conclusion: 将VLM与专家先验通过智能体编排相结合，能够以更低的标注成本复现专家定义的标注流程，同时保持可解释性。

Abstract: Human-labeled data are widely used as reference annotations in ML, despite known variability across annotators in many expert-driven domains. In addition, expert annotation is slow, inconsistent, and remains a major bottleneck for scaling tasks like tree height bias classification in forestry remote sensing. We propose a multi-agent system (MAS) that orchestrates expert decision trees with Vision-Language Models (VLMs), treating the decision tree as a structural prior while VLMs perform localized semantic perception at individual nodes, with multi-agent voting to mitigate VLM stochasticity. We formalize a Decoupled Declarative Decision (D3) Framework that enables zero-modification generalization across diverse expert-defined decision structures. On a tree bias classification testbed, our framework outperforms supervised ML baselines and reduces the amount of expert labeling effort required. These results suggest that agentic orchestration of VLMs with expert priors can reproduce expert-defined labeling procedures at substantially lower annotation cost while maintaining interpretability.

</details>


### [160] [Self-Study Reconsidered: The Hidden Fragility of Learning from Self-Generated QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32002)
*Ekaterina Alimaskina,Denis Shveykin,Gleb Molodtsov,Igor Shalygin,Alexey Kadeishvili,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了从合成QA数据训练语言模型时，生成阶段并非中性预处理，而是一种隐性策略，存在选择偏差（集中于显著区域、易被格式噪声劫持）和回答偏差（模型倾向于服从嵌入文本中的指令式段落）两类脆弱性。通过将问题绑定到固定目标（减少选择偏差）和过滤指令式文本（将注入顺从率从88%降至13%），可缓解这些问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成QA数据广泛用于微调、蒸馏和知识压缩，但生成阶段的偏差被忽视。作者希望揭示QA生成作为隐性策略对训练信号的影响，并证明其脆弱性。

Method: 通过实验分析QA生成的两个阶段（选择和回答）的偏差模式，并提出两种缓解策略：将问题绑定到固定目标以减少选择偏差，过滤指令式文本降低回答注入风险。

Result: 生成器对文档覆盖不均匀、集中于显著区域；格式噪声可劫持问题生成；模型倾向于服从文本中指令式段落，且任务冲突下更大模型遵从率更高。缓解措施将注入顺从率从88%降至13%，同时保留几乎全部干净文本。

Conclusion: 合成QA生成存在系统性偏差，但通过针对性预处理可有效缓解，无需改变训练循环。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly taught from synthetic question--answer (QA) supervision: a model generates questions about a document, answers them from the same text, and the resulting pairs are used to fine-tune, distill, or compress knowledge into another model. We show that this generation step is not neutral preprocessing. It is an implicit policy that both selects which evidence becomes training signal and decides how that evidence is answered, and it is fragile at both stages. When choosing what to ask, generators do not scan a document uniformly. Coverage saturates early and concentrates on salient spans, diverse prompts converge on the same regions, and what looks question-worthy is driven by local presentation. As a result, salient artifacts such as poorly cleaned markup can hijack question generation across model families and scales. When answering, the model that produces the supervision tends to obey instruction-like passages embedded in the text. This compliance depends on the intent and surface form of the passage rather than its strictness, and is worst under task conflict, where larger models comply more often. These failure modes arise from choices made during QA generation, so they can be reduced without changing the training loop. Tying each question to a fixed target reduces biased selection, and filtering instruction-like spans before answering lowers mean injection compliance from $88\%$ to $13\%$ in our evaluation while retaining nearly all clean text.

</details>


### [161] [PolicyGuard: From Organizational Policies to Neuro-SymbolicCompliance Review Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32004)
*Sameer Malik,Ayush Singh,Amar Prakash Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolicyGuard是一个神经符号框架，将组织政策转化为可执行的审核引擎，结合LLM的局部文档解释和符号规则评估，实现文档合规性审查的显式化、可维护和可测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端的LLM提示将策略逻辑隐含在模型中，导致合规决策难以检查、更新和测试。

Method: 将组织政策转换为包含类型化关系逻辑规则和原子级提取问题的可执行审核引擎，LLM回答局部问题，符号评估器应用形式化规则检测不合规。

Result: 在NDA合规审查任务上进行了实例化和评估，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过分离策略形式化、局部文档解释和符号合规评估，使文档审查更显式、可维护且可系统测试。

Abstract: Policy-grounded document review requires determining whether a target document complies with organization-specific policies, guidelines, or playbooks. While large language models can assist with policy interpretation and document analysis, end-to-end prompting leaves the applied policy logic implicit, making compliance decisions difficult to inspect, update, and test. We present PolicyGuard, a neuro-symbolic framework for policy-grounded document compliance review. PolicyGuard converts organizational policy guidance into an executable review engine consisting of typed relational logic rules and atom-level extraction questions. During review, LLMs answer these local questions using retrieved document evidence, and a symbolic evaluator applies the formal rules to detect non-compliance. We instantiate and evaluate PolicyGuard on company-specific NDA compliance review, where contract clauses must be checked against organization-specific negotiation policies. By separating policy formalization, local document interpretation, and symbolic compliance evaluation, PolicyGuard makes document review more explicit, maintainable, and systematically testable.

</details>


### [162] [AxDafny: Agentic Verified Code Generation in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32007)
*Benjamin Breen,Austin Letson,Borja Requena Pozo,Leopoldo Sarra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AxDafny，一个验证器引导的修复框架，用于在 Dafny 中生成可执行代码和证明工件（不变量、断言、终止参数）。在 LCB-Pro-Dafny 基准（250 道竞赛题）上，AxDafny 相比 GPT-5.5 基线显著提升验证成功率；在 DafnyBench 上达到 92.7% 的验证成功率，比最强的证明提示基线高出 6.5 个百分点。此外，验证成功与运行时测试性能衡量代码的不同方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成模型在 Dafny 这类需要形式验证的语言中表现不佳，因为不仅要生成可执行代码，还需提供证明工件（不变量、断言、终止参数）以保证程序正确性。缺乏验证器引导的迭代修复机制，难以自动生成满足验证要求的完整代码。

Method: 提出 AxDafny 框架，采用验证器引导的修复循环：迭代生成 Dafny 实现代码、不变量、断言和终止参数，利用验证器反馈进行自动修复。同时构建 LCB-Pro-Dafny 基准，包含 250 道竞赛编程题的 Dafny 翻译版本，配有形式化规范和基于验证器的评估工具。

Result: 在 LCB-Pro-Dafny 上，AxDafny 显著超过 GPT-5.5 基线。在 DafnyBench 上达到 92.7% 验证成功率，比之前最强的证明提示基线高 6.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 验证成功与运行时测试性能衡量代码的不同方面，表明形式验证和传统测试互补。

Abstract: We study agentic code generation in Dafny, where a model must generate both executable code and the proof artifacts for verification. We present AxDafny, a verifier-guided repair framework that iteratively generates implementations, invariants, assertions, and termination arguments. We also introduce LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny (LCB-Pro-Dafny), a benchmark of 250 competition-style programming problems translated into Dafny with formal specifications and a verifier-based evaluation harness. On LCB-Pro-Dafny, AxDafny substantially improves verification success over baseline GPT-5.5 performance. On DafnyBench, AxDafny achieves 92.7\% verification success, outperforming the strongest previously reported proof-hint baseline by 6.5 percentage points. Lastly, we show that verification success and runtime test performance measure different aspects of generated code.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [On the structure of optimal free Dirichlet regions in mass transportation problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30826)
*Lucas D. O'Brien*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧支撑概率测度μ在ℝ^d上的平均距离问题，证明了Buttazzo、Oudet和Stepanov关于最小化子拓扑结构猜想的更一般形式，特别在所有维度上验证了原作者最初研究情形下的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题由Buttazzo、Oudet和Stepanov于2002年提出，用于研究传输成本可为零的自由区域上的最优传输问题。本文旨在证明他们提出的关于平均距离问题最小化子拓扑结构的猜想。

Method: 基于Kobayashi、Kim和作者之前关于重心的研究，使用重心场（barycentre field）工具，对最小化子的拓扑结构进行更一般性的证明。

Result: 在更大 generality 上证明了Buttazzo、Oudet和Stepanov关于平均距离问题最小化子的拓扑描述猜想，特别是在所有维度下验证了原作者最初研究的情形。

Conclusion: 本文证明了平均距离问题最小化子的拓扑结构猜想，为该领域提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: For a compactly supported probability measure $μ$ on the $d$-dimensional space $\mathbb{R}^d$, the average distance problem asks us to minimize the average distance functional over all compact, connected, $Σ\subseteq \mathbb{R}^d$ satisfying the Hausdorff $1$-measure constraint $\mathcal{H}^1(Σ) \leq \ell$. This problem was first introduced in 2002 by Buttazzo, Oudet, and Stepanov to study optimal transport problems with free regions on which the transport cost vanishes, and has undergone a considerable amount of research since. Most recently, Kobayashi, Kim, and the author studied the structure of these regions using the barycentre field, a tool for studying the average distance functional introduced previously by Kobayashi, Hayase, and Kim. In this paper, we build upon this work to prove in much greater generality a topological description of minimizers of the average distance problem conjectured by Buttazzo, Oudet, and Stepanov. In particular, we prove this conjecture in all dimensions in the case originally studied by these authors.

</details>


### [164] [Joint Chance Constrained Safe-Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30829)
*Niklas Schmid,Jared Miller,Tristan Zeller,Marta Fochesato,Tobias Sutter,John Lygeros*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机系统联合机会约束最优控制问题，提出仅最小化安全轨迹期望成本的方法，通过增广状态空间的约束马尔可夫决策过程建模，利用动态规划求解，并推导了网格近似下策略安全性的界，在2D独轮车仿真中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联合机会约束最优控制同时最小化安全与不安全轨迹的期望成本，导致策略倾向于利用低成本的不安全轨迹来违反约束。当约束代表关键失效状态（如无人机坠毁）时，这种不可取行为需要被克服。

Method: 提出仅最小化安全轨迹期望成本的优化框架，将问题建模为增广状态空间上的约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），通过动态规划求解；针对连续状态空间，推导网格近似下策略安全性的理论界。

Result: 在2D独轮车系统的杂乱到达-避障仿真环境中，通过动态规划和强化学习方法进行了实验比较。

Conclusion: 仅最小化安全轨迹期望成本能有效避免传统方法中邀请约束违规的行为，为安全关键系统中的机会约束最优控制提供了更合理的框架。

Abstract: We consider the finite-time optimal control of stochastic systems subject to a probabilistic constraint on the trajectories' safety. Such formulations are known as joint chance constrained optimal control problems. The common practice is to jointly minimise the expected cost of all trajectories, safe and unsafe. This leads to policies which invite constraint violations to exploit low-cost unsafe trajectories. When constraints represent states of critical failure, such behaviour is undesirable. We demonstrate that this behaviour can be overcome by only minimizing the expected cost of safe trajectories. The underlying rationale follows a practical intuition: In many applications, the cost incurred by unsafe trajectories is irrelevant (e.g., the battery usage of a crashed quadcopter), and one is usually interested in minimizing the cost of trajectories that are safe. We show that this problem can be cast as a constrained Markov Decision Process over an augmented state space. This allows solving it via dynamic programming. We derive bounds on the policies' safety under errors resulting from gridding approximations when the system's state space is continuous. Finally, we empirically compare dynamic programming as well as reinforcement learning solutions on a simulated 2D unicycle system in cluttered reach-avoid environments.

</details>


### [165] [$\texttt{bucket-graph-spprc}$: an extensible C++ library for the shortest path problem with resource constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30847)
*Simon Spoorendonk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源、仅头文件的C++23库bgspprc，用于求解带资源约束的最短路径问题（SPPRC），该库实现了桶图标记算法，通过编译期资源概念设计实现零运行时开销的资源组合，在公开实例上相比PathWyse加速1.3-2.35倍，性能接近并行拉取标记算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPPRC是分支切割定价算法的核心子问题，现有开源实现性能不足，需要高效、可扩展且易用的库来支持车辆路径规划及相关问题的求解。

Method: 实现Sadykov等人(2021)的桶图标记算法，包含双向标记、跨弧拼接、桶固定和弧消除；采用结构体数组(SoA)标签存储和SIMD加速的支配检查；核心设计是编译期资源概念——新资源只需实现七个函数接口，资源组合成标签状态时无运行时派发，布局在编译期确定；内置五种资源：时间/容量、ng路径松弛、秩1割、累计成本、取送货。

Result: 在共享公开实例上，bgspprc比PathWyse快1.3-2.35倍（移位几何平均），单线程运行时仍快1.3-2.3倍；性能在1.9-2.4倍内接近并行拉取标记算法。

Conclusion: bgspprc提供了高效、可扩展的SPPRC求解方案，代码和基准测试均已公开，可复现。

Abstract: We present $\texttt{bucket-graph-spprc}$ ($\texttt{bgspprc}$ for short), an open-source, header-only C++23 library for the shortest path problem with resource constraints (SPPRC), the pricing subproblem at the heart of branch-cut-and-price for vehicle routing and related problems. The library implements the bucket-graph labelling algorithm of Sadykov, Uchoa and Pessoa (2021), with bidirectional labelling, across-arc concatenation, bucket fixing and arc elimination, and a structure-of-arrays label store with SIMD-accelerated dominance. Its central design feature is a compile-time resource concept: a new SPPRC variant is added by implementing a fixed seven-function interface, and resources compose into a label state with no runtime dispatch, the state layout fixed at compile time. Five resources ship built in: time/capacity, ng-path elementarity relaxation, rank-1 cuts, cumulative cost, and pickup-and-delivery. In a reproducible, head-to-head comparison on shared public instances at an identical bound, $\texttt{bgspprc}$ outperforms PathWyse (Salani, Basso and Giuffrida, 2024), the main open-source comparator, by $1.3\times$--$2.35\times$ in shifted geometric mean (and by $1.3\times$--$2.3\times$ even when itself run single-threaded), and runs within $1.9\times$--$2.4\times$ of parallel pull labelling (Petersen and Spoorendonk, 2025), a different labelling technique for the same problem. The library, benchmark scripts, and pinned instances are publicly available.

</details>


### [166] [Optimal Design of Tubular Perfectly Conducting Objects in Electromagnetic Chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30891)
*Tilo Arens,Roland Griesmaier,Marvin Knöller,Raphael Schurr*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了长管状物体在电磁手性中的形状优化问题，通过牛顿型迭代方法最大化正则化电磁手性度量，基于电场积分方程和边界元方法实现了形状优化算法，生成了能激发高阶模式的高手性散射体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁手性描述了散射体对不同偏振手性的电磁波的差异化响应，而如何通过形状优化获得强手性散射体是重要问题，现有研究尚未充分解决远场算子设置下的域导数分析。

Method: 采用牛顿型迭代方法最大化正则化电磁手性度量；严格分析了物体到远场算子映射的可微性，将域导数结果推广到远场算子设置；基于电场积分方程进行散射场评估和域导数计算，通过边界元方法实现数值算法。

Result: 数值实验生成了强电磁手性散射体，其形状能激发超越偶极子 regime 的高阶模式，扩展了已知的高手性散射体集合。

Conclusion: 本文提出的形状优化方法能够有效生成具有非直观形状的强电磁手性散射体，为设计新型手性超材料和散射体提供了理论基础和数值框架。

Abstract: This work is about the shape optimization of long tubular objects in electromagnetic chirality (em-chirality). Em-chirality is a property of individual scattering objects or metamaterials describing their qualitatively different response to electromagnetic waves of opposite polarization handedness. The optimization is performed by a Newton-type iterative maximization of a regularized em-chirality measure with respect to the scatterer's shape. In this context, the differentiability of the object-to-far field operator map is analyzed rigorously, thereby extending previously known results on the domain derivative to the far field operator setting. Our optimal design algorithm is based on the electric field integral equation, which is employed both for the evaluation of scattered fields and for the computation of the domain derivative. Our implementation is done via the boundary element method. The numerical examples presented in this work yield strongly em-chiral scattering objects capable of exciting higher-order modes beyond the dipole regime with nonintuitive shapes that expand the known set of highly em-chiral scattering objects.

</details>


### [167] [Fuel-Optimal Low-Thrust Trajectory Design under High-Fidelity Dynamics: A State Transition Matrix-Based Sensitivity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30933)
*Liqiang Hou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于STM灵敏度分析的间接法，用于在高保真动力学下设计燃料最优的低推力转移轨道。该方法将协态方程重构为仅含状态变量及其时间导数的常微分方程，将高阶动力学效应视为黑箱组件，避免了推导系统动力学偏导数的复杂过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统间接法求解燃料最优低推力转移时，需要推导系统动力学的显式偏导数表达式以构建协态方程，这一过程在高保真动力学（包含多种摄动效应）下变得极其复杂。为解决这一难题，本文提出了一种无需显式推导偏导数的替代方法。

Method: 提出基于状态转移矩阵（STM）灵敏度分析的间接法，将协态方程重构为仅涉及状态变量及其时间导数的常微分方程；将高阶动力学效应（太阳辐射压、J2扁率、木星引力、相对论效应）视为黑箱组件；利用标准梯度优化器或内点优化器求解最优协态和转移参数。

Result: 通过经典地球-火星转移场景验证了该方法与传统间接法的等价性；成功应用于包含多种摄动的高保真动力学地球-火星转移；进一步扩展到多圈地球-金星转移高保真动力学场景。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持间接法精度的同时，显著简化了高保真动力学下燃料最优转移轨道设计的推导复杂度，具有良好的通用性和计算效率。

Abstract: A straightforward and computationally efficient indirect method based on STM sensitivity analysis is introduced for designing fuel-optimal low-thrust transfers under high-fidelity dynamics. Conventional indirect approaches require explicit expressions for the partial derivatives of the system dynamics to formulate the costate equations, making the derivation process complex for high-fidelity trajectory design. In this work, the costate equations are reformulated as ordinary differential equations involving only the state variables and their time derivatives. High-order dynamical effects are treated as black-box components, avoiding the need to derive partial derivatives of the system dynamics. A standard gradient-based or interior-point optimizer is used to determine the optimal costates and transfer parameters. The equivalence between the proposed method and conventional approaches is demonstrated through a classic Earth-Mars transfer scenario. An Earth-Mars transfer under high-fidelity dynamics is then presented, including perturbations from solar radiation pressure, solar J2 oblateness, Jupiter third-body gravity, and relativistic effects. Finally, the method is applied to a multiple-revolution Earth-Venus transfer under high-fidelity dynamics.

</details>


### [168] [Difference-of-Convex Optimization via Inexact Smoothing Descent Methods: Difference of High-Order Moreau Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30991)
*Alireza Kabgani,Moslem Zamani,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究通过平滑下降技术求解差凸（DC）优化问题。引入高阶Moreau包络差（HOME-DC），建立其基本性质和微分性质。通过近似底层近端点，生成HOME-DC的近似一阶预言机并刻画其精度保证。基于此预言机，提出一类用于最小化DC函数的不精确下降方法并提供收敛性分析。该框架将基于包络的优化技术推广到广泛的结构化非凸问题，同时允许子问题的不精确求解。稀疏聚类问题的初步数值实验验证了方法的实际潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DC优化问题在机器学习等领域广泛应用，但现有方法在处理结构化非凸问题时存在局限，且通常要求子问题精确求解，限制了实际应用。本文旨在通过平滑下降技术和高阶Moreau包络，建立更灵活、允许不精确求解的DC优化框架。

Method: 提出HOME-DC（高阶Moreau包络差）概念并分析其性质；通过近似近端点构造HOME-DC的不精确一阶预言机并刻画误差界；基于该预言机设计不精确下降方法；在稀疏聚类问题上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 建立了HOME-DC的基本与微分性质；刻画了不精确一阶预言机的精度保证；给出了所提不精确下降方法的收敛性分析；稀疏聚类数值实验展示了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文所提的HOME-DC框架成功将基于包络的优化技术推广到更广泛的DC优化问题，允许子问题的不精确求解，理论分析与数值实验共同验证了方法的合理性与潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies difference-of-convex (DC) optimization problems through smoothing descent techniques. In particular, we introduce the difference of high-order Moreau envelopes (HOME-DC) and establish its fundamental and differential properties. Approximating the underlying proximal points, we generate an inexact first-order oracle for HOME-DC and characterize its accuracy guarantees. Building upon this oracle, we propose a class of inexact descent methods for minimizing DC functions and provide a convergence analysis. The proposed framework extends the applicability of envelope-based optimization techniques to a broad class of structured nonconvex problems while accommodating inexact solutions to subproblems. Preliminary numerical experiments on a sparse clustering problem demonstrate the approach's practical potential and support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [169] [Augmenting airline networks using airside-to-airside buses to strengthen system resilience under disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31142)
*Micah M. Borrero,Max Z. Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引入空侧到空侧的巴士线路，将短途区域航班转换为低成本、高频次的巴士服务，对航空运输网络进行拓扑重构，以提升网络韧性。基于代理的仿真表明，在1000万美元预算下将10条区域航线转换为巴士服务，可使中断日和正常日的旅客延误分别减少8%和6%。敏感性分析显示，扩大巴士参数（里程和投资）会带来延误缓解的边际收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空运输网络的中断每年导致数百万旅客滞留和数十亿美元收入损失。现有研究多关注中断后的反应性措施，缺乏主动提升网络韧性的策略。本文旨在通过低成本方式重构网络拓扑，从根源上增强系统抗干扰能力。

Method: 构建网络增广模型，将空侧到空侧的巴士线路作为短途区域航班的替代方案融入现有航空网络；通过基于代理的仿真在正常和中断条件下评估旅客平均小时延误变化；进行敏感性分析考察巴士参数（里程和投资预算）对延误缓解效果的影响。

Result: 在1000万美元预算约束下，将10条区域航线转为巴士服务，中断日旅客延误减少8%，正常日减少6%。扩大巴士参数范围可降低运营成本，但延误缓解呈现边际收益递减。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为航空公司、机场和监管机构提供了一个决策支持工具，用于将多式联运策略整合到未来的中断管理政策中。研究还讨论了现实先例以及实施面临的监管和政治障碍。

Abstract: Each year, disruptions in the air transportation network strand millions of passengers and cost airlines billions in revenue. Airline networks prioritize operational and cost efficiency through hub-and-spoke structures that maximize revenue; however, these hubs also act as critical choke points during disruptions. Previous studies have focused on reactionary measures in response to air transportation network disruptions, whereas this work proposes a proactive strategy to improve resilience by reconfiguring the network's topology. Specifically, we consider airside-to-airside bus lines as a low-cost, frequent alternative to short, regional flights, offering service that can circumvent air traffic-related delays. We develop a network construction model that augments the existing air transportation network with these bus lines. The augmented networks are analyzed through an agent-based simulation, where increased resilience is measured in terms of decreased average hourly passenger delays under both nominal and disrupted conditions. Our results demonstrate that converting 10 regional routes from air service to airside-to-airside bus service, for a baseline scenario that is constrained by a $10 million investment budget, can reduce passenger delays by an average of 8% on disrupted days and 6% on nominal days. Furthermore, through a sensitivity analysis, we show that while augmenting the system using these buses decreases operational costs compared to the historic air-only network, continuously expanding bus parameters (i.e., range and investment budget) yields diminishing returns in delay mitigation. Finally, we discuss real-world precedents alongside regulatory and political hurdles to implementation. The proposed framework offers airlines, airports, and regulators a decision-support tool for integrating multimodal strategies into future disruption management policies.

</details>


### [170] [Direction-Magnitude Decomposition for Low-Rank Matrix Optimization: Faster Convergence and Saddle-to-saddle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31390)
*Yudong Wei,Liang Zhang,Bingcong Li,Niao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出方向-幅度分解（DMD）框架，将低秩矩阵优化问题分解为方向和幅度两部分，并开发了过参数化DMD（使用大秩实现更快收敛）和递归DMD（基于鞍点-鞍点学习行为，降低计算成本）两种方法，在矩阵分解、感知和补全任务上比Burer-Monteiro梯度下降快指数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burer-Monteiro（BM）公式中因子化秩r的选择敏感且会显著降低优化效率，现有方法在目标秩未知时性能不佳。

Method: 提出方向-幅度分解（DMD）统一框架，将优化变量分解为方向和幅度两部分。基于此开发两种方法：(1) 过参数化DMD，使用比必要秩更大的r，r越大收敛越快；(2) 递归DMD，受过参数化DMD的增量特征对学习/鞍点-鞍点行为启发，具有更低的存储和计算开销。

Result: 两种DMD方法在矩阵分解上均比BM公式上的梯度下降快指数级；过参数化DMD随r增大收敛加速；递归DMD在保持性能的同时降低计算成本；数值实验在矩阵分解、感知和补全任务上验证了理论发现和实际有效性。

Conclusion: DMD框架有效解决了BM公式中秩选择的难题，在目标秩未知时仍能高效优化，两种变体各有优势，为低秩矩阵优化提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Low-rank matrix optimization is often carried out via the Burer-Monteiro (BM) formulation, but choosing the factorization rank $r$ is delicate and can substantially slow optimization. We propose a unified framework, termed direction-magnitude decomposition (DMD), that decomposes the optimization variable to improve optimization efficiency even when the target rank is unknown. We develop two DMD-based approaches and establish their theoretical advantages on the canonical problem of matrix factorization. The first, overparameterized DMD, uses a rank $r$ larger than necessary and enjoys faster convergence as $r$ increases. The second, recursive DMD, is motivated by the incremental eigenpair learning, or saddle-to-saddle, behavior of overparameterized DMD. It achieves lower memory and computational costs, complementing overparameterized DMD. Both approaches are exponentially faster than gradient descent applied to the BM formulation. Numerical experiments on matrix factorization, sensing, and completion corroborate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of DMD.

</details>


### [171] [Joint Planning and Scheduling of Modular Vehicles for Passenger-Freight Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31425)
*Wanru Chen,Jiaming Wu,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向客货混行双向公交走廊的模块化车辆系统，通过可重构的同质单元实现客运与货运的动态集成运营，并构建了基于时空状态网络的随机混合整数规划模型，采用Benders分解算法与启发式方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公交系统难以灵活应对不确定的乘客需求，且客货运输分离导致资源利用率低。本文旨在通过模块化车辆的可重构特性，实现客运与货运的动态协同，提升运营效率与资源利用率。

Method: 采用同质单元组成可重构车辆，在中间站进行解编与重组以响应不确定需求；构建时空状态网络模型，将发车时刻表、车辆编组、单元调度及客货需求分配联合优化；通过线性化机会约束处理乘客需求不确定性；设计精确Benders分解算法（含有效不等式与热启动策略）及基于分解的启发式算法。

Result: 在哥德堡公交走廊实例上，Benders算法在小中型算例中高效求解，启发式算法在大规模问题中具备可扩展性；敏感性分析揭示了考虑需求不确定性的价值以及客货需求时间重叠的影响；与基准公交系统的对比验证了所提集成系统的运营优势。

Conclusion: 模块化客货混行车辆系统在不确定需求下能显著降低单元部署成本与乘客等待成本，系统集成运营优于传统分离式方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a modular vehicle system for passenger-freight integration along a bidirectional transit corridor. The system uses homogeneous units that can be coupled into vehicles and assigned to either passenger or freight service. Freight is carried by dedicated units, with loading and unloading coordinated with docking and undocking and separated from passenger boarding and alighting. To better respond to uncertain passenger demand and integrate freight transport, vehicles can be reconfigured at intermediate stations, where they can also depart and terminate. We jointly optimize departure-specific service routes, timetables, vehicle compositions, unit schedules, and passenger-freight demand assignments, with unit reuse constrained by explicit docking and undocking times. These decisions are modeled on a space-time-state network and formulated as a stochastic mixed-integer program that minimizes unit deployment costs, passenger waiting costs, and penalties for unmet freight demand. Passenger demand uncertainty is addressed using linearized chance constraints. To solve the problem, we develop an exact Benders decomposition algorithm with valid inequalities and a warm-start strategy, together with a tailored decomposition-based heuristic for larger instances. Computational experiments on instances generated from representative transit corridors in Gothenburg demonstrate the effectiveness of the Benders algorithm for small- and medium-sized instances and the scalability of the heuristic for larger problems. Sensitivity analyses highlight the value of accounting for passenger demand uncertainty and the effects of temporal overlap between passenger and freight demand. Comparisons with benchmark transit systems further demonstrate the operational advantages of the proposed modular integrated system.

</details>


### [172] [Exponential rate of convergence of relative value iteration algorithms for ergodic controls of diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31431)
*Sumith Reddy Anugu,Guodong Pang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散过程在一致指数稳定条件下，针对传统遍历成本和遍历风险敏感成本准则的相对值迭代（RVI）算法的收敛速度，证明了在加权上确界范数下RVI算法具有指数收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的RVI算法虽能渐进收敛到最优值，但其收敛速率问题尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出基于偏微分方程微调的高散时间RVI实现，将迭代格式构造为显式递归系统；在CEC情形下利用马尔可夫核表示迭代与值函数的差异，在ERSC情形下对相应量的对数进行类似处理；通过加权半范数证明马尔可夫核的压缩性，进而建立指数收敛速率。

Result: 证明了所提离散时间RVI算法在加权上确界范数下具有指数收敛速率，且CEC和ERSC两种准则下均成立。

Conclusion: 通过马尔可夫核的压缩性质与加权半范数技术，成功建立了扩散过程RVI算法的指数收敛速率理论。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the rate of convergence of the relative value iteration (RVI) algorithms for diffusions in $\mathbb{R}^d$ under both the conventional ergodic cost (CEC) and ergodic risk-sensitive cost (ERSC) criteria, and under the uniform exponential stability condition. The existing RVI algorithms for the CEC and ERSC problems solve the associated initial value Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman type equations whose solutions are shown to converge asymptotically to the corresponding optimal values. However, the rates of convergence for such algorithms have remained open. This paper proposes discrete-time implementations for the RVI algorithms based on slight modifications of the associated PDEs, and proves that the rates of convergence of these RVI algorithms are exponential under a weighted sup-norm. These implementations have discrete-time iterates that can be explicitly expressed as recursive systems. The difference between these iterates and the desired value function in the CEC case can then be expressed in terms of the associated Markov kernels. Similarly, this can be done for the logarithms of the corresponding iterates and desired value function in the ERSC case in terms of the associated Markov kernels for the extended diffusion. As a result, we are able to prove the desirable contraction properties in order to establish the exponential rate of convergence by making use of a weighted semi-norm in which Markov kernel acts a contraction.

</details>


### [173] [ILPU: Iterative Laplace-Based Phase Unwrapping via Bi-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31460)
*Michael Fedders,Jakob Schattenfroh,Yanglei Wu,Noah Jaitner,Tom Meyer,Jakob Jordan,Jing Guo,Ingolf Sack,Hossein S. Aghamiry*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双层优化的迭代拉普拉斯相位解缠算法（ILPU），通过DCT求解增量泊松方程与质量引导空间正则化相结合的框架，在保持O(N log N)计算复杂度的同时，实现了对低信噪比区域的鲁棒解缠，在3D脑MRI相位图像上相对误差仅2.12%，显著优于SEGUE（67.59%）和Laplace（81.02%）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位解缠是磁共振相位成像（如磁敏感成像、场图成像、温度测量、磁共振弹性成像）的关键预处理步骤，现有方法在低信噪比区域和三维体积解缠中精度不足，需要一种既鲁棒又高效的计算方案。

Method: 提出ILPU双层优化算法：下层求解器通过离散余弦变换（DCT）求解增量泊松方程恢复连续相位增量；上层求解器通过质量引导的空间正则化和受限局部搜索优化整数偏移图。两层耦合通过自适应平滑惩罚和质量加权正则化实现对低SNR区域的鲁棒解缠。

Result: 在2D和3D脑MRI相位图像上，以人工解缠参考数据为金标准，ILPU在2D中精度与SEGUE相当；在3D中相对误差为2.12%，远优于SEGUE（67.59%）和Laplace（81.02%）。算法每轮迭代复杂度为O(N log N)，计算速度优于Flynn和SEGUE。

Conclusion: 双层优化框架为MRI相位解缠提供了一种鲁棒且计算高效的解决方案，特别在三维体积解缠中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Phase unwrapping is an essential preprocessing step for phase-based MRI applications, including susceptibility mapping, field mapping, thermometry, and MR elastography. We present Iterative Laplace-Based Phase Unwrapping (ILPU), a bi-level optimization algorithm. In this method, a lower-level solver recovers a continuous phase increment from an incremental Poisson equation using the discrete cosine transform (DCT), while an upper-level solver refines an integer offset map through quality-guided spatial regularization and a restricted local search.
  This coupling enables robust unwrapping in low-SNR regions through adaptive smoothness penalties and quality-weighted regularization. We evaluated ILPU on 2D and 3D brain MRI phase images against manually unwrapped reference data, using standard Laplace unwrapping, Flynn, and SEGUE as comparison methods. In 2D, ILPU achieves accuracy comparable to SEGUE. In 3D, ILPU attains a relative error of 2.12% compared with 67.59% for SEGUE and 81.02% for Laplace, demonstrating a clear advantage in volumetric unwrapping.
  The algorithm has O(N log N) complexity per iteration through DCT-based Laplacian estimation and is numerically faster than both Flynn and SEGUE while preserving superior accuracy. These results indicate that the bi-level optimization framework provides a robust and computationally efficient solution for phase unwrapping in MRI.

</details>


### [174] [A Geometry-Adaptive Regularized Newton-Type Method for Manifold-Affine Intersection Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31738)
*Dengyu Zheng,Shixiang Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出正则化牛顿SLRA（RN-SLRA）算法，用于解决弱横截条件下流形-仿射子空间交集问题。通过引入正则化项克服牛顿SLRA在横截条件失效时的缺陷，证明在内蕴横截条件下线性收敛，在横截条件下达到高阶（含二次）收敛，并推广到非精确投影变体。数值实验验证了其在退化情形下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿SLRA是求解流形-仿射子空间交集问题的有效方法，但其良定义性依赖于流形与仿射子空间之间的横截条件，该条件在实际应用中可能不成立，导致算法失效。因此需要一种在弱横截条件下仍能稳定工作的正则化方法。

Method: RN-SLRA通过在牛顿SLRA的迭代中引入正则化项，克服了横截条件缺失带来的奇异性问题。文章分析了两种收敛性：在内蕴横截条件下线性收敛，在标准横截条件下通过适当选择正则化参数可实现二次收敛。同时研究了非精确投影变体，用近似投影代替精确投影，并证明收敛性质得以保持。

Result: 理论证明了RN-SLRA在内蕴横截条件下的线性收敛性，以及在横截条件下的高阶（含二次）收敛性。非精确投影变体同样保持这些收敛性质。数值实验在构造的退化实例和Hankel结构示例上表明，RN-SLRA在牛顿SLRA可能失败的场景中具有显著改善的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RN-SLRA通过正则化策略有效拓展了牛顿SLRA的适用范围，在横截条件不满足时仍能保证收敛，且保持了高阶收敛速率。该算法为结构低秩近似等实际问题提供了更可靠的求解工具。

Abstract: We propose a regularized algorithm, Regularized Newton-SLRA (RN-SLRA), for local manifold--affine intersection problems under weak intersection conditions, motivated in particular by structured low-rank approximation (SLRA). Newton-SLRA is an efficient method for manifold--affine intersection problems, but its well-definedness relies on the transversality condition between the manifold and the affine subspace, a condition that may fail in practice. RN-SLRA overcomes this difficulty by introducing a regularization term. We prove that, under the intrinsic transversality condition, RN-SLRA converges linearly to the intersection, while under the transversality condition it achieves higher-order convergence, including quadratic convergence for a suitable choice of the regularization parameter. We also study an inexact-projection variant, in which the projection onto the manifold is computed approximately, and show that the same local linear and quadratic convergence properties are preserved under the corresponding assumptions. Numerical experiments on constructed degenerate instances and Hankel-structured examples illustrate improved robustness in settings where Newton-SLRA may fail.

</details>


### [175] [Explicit Series and a Certified Hybrid Evaluator for the $\ell_p$ Proximity Operator for $0<p<1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31951)
*Lixin Shen,Jiangyu Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸ℓ_p拟范数(0<p<1)的邻近算子问题，通过Lagrange-Bürmann反演级数和Mellin-Barnes积分表示，设计了具有可计算先验误差界的认证混合评估器，并针对有理数p给出了闭式解，最后将其集成到非精确邻近梯度法中并证明收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸ℓ_p拟范数(0<p<1)是强大的稀疏性代理，但其邻近算子prox_{λ|·|^p}的鲁棒评估十分困难，亟需精确且数值稳定的计算方法。

Method: 1) 给出标量邻近映射的显式刻画，包括阈值结构和严格孤立解的条件；2) 应用Lagrange-Bürmann反演推导一致收敛级数表示较大正根；3) 导出Mellin-Barnes积分表示并解释收敛半径；4) 设计认证混合评估器(短级数+截断垂直MB段)，提供可计算先验误差界；5) 对有理数p，利用Gauss乘法公式将系数简化为有限乘积，将级数重组为广义超几何函数有限和。

Result: 给出了p∈{1/3,1/2,2/3}时的闭式解；集成的非精确邻近梯度法在证书可求和条件下证明收敛；MATLAB数值验证了精度，包括近阈值行为。

Conclusion: 本文为ℓ_p拟范数邻近算子提供了理论上精确、数值上稳定的计算框架，尤其解决了近阈值区域的精度问题，并可用于非精确优化算法。

Abstract: The nonconvex $\ell_p$ quasi-norm with $0<p<1$ is a powerful sparsity surrogate but makes the proximity operator $\mathrm{prox}_{λ|\cdot|^p}$ nontrivial to evaluate robustly. We give an explicit characterization of the scalar proximal map for all $0<p<1$, including the threshold structure and conditions ensuring strict, isolated solutions. Applying the Lagrange--Bürmann inversion to the stationarity equation yields a uniformly convergent series for the larger positive root, which provides an exact and numerically stable formula above the classical threshold. We further derive a Mellin--Barnes (MB) integral representation, explaining its radius of convergence and enabling certified truncation. Building on these ingredients, we design a {certified hybrid evaluator} (short series $+$ truncated vertical MB segment) with a computable a priori error bound that remains accurate in the near-threshold regime. For rational $p$, Gauss' multiplication formula reduces the coefficients to finite products of shifted Gamma functions, reorganizing the series into a finite sum of generalized hypergeometric functions and explaining the closed forms at $p\in\{1/3,1/2,2/3\}$. We integrate the evaluator into a proximal-gradient method with an inexact proximal oracle and prove convergence under standard summability of the certificates; MATLAB implementations and numerics confirm accuracy, including near-threshold behavior.

</details>


### [176] [Approximate Controllability of the generalized Burgers-Huxley equation in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31968)
*Aman Patel,Mohmedmunavvar Mubarak Bapu,Mrinmay Biswas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Burgers-Huxley (GBH)方程通过内部控制的能控性，证明了该方程无法全局近似能控，但可以通过局部内部控制将系统从一个稳态驱动到另一个稳态的任意小邻域内，前提是这两个稳态位于同一连通分量中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBH方程是描述反应、对流和扩散相互作用的典型模型，探索其能控性对于理解和操纵这类非线性偏微分方程所描述的物理系统具有重要意义。

Method: 采用支持在定义域任意非空开子集上的内部控制，通过数学分析研究GBH方程的能控性性质，特别关注稳态之间的转移控制问题。

Result: (1) GBH方程在给定时间内不是全局近似能控的；(2) 在适当时间内，可以通过局部内部控制将系统从任一稳态驱动到另一稳态的任意小邻域，前提是两个稳态位于同一连通分量中。

Conclusion: GBH方程的能控性存在限制：全局近似能控不可实现，但同一连通分量内的稳态间可以通过局部控制实现任意精度的转移。

Abstract: The generalized Burgers-Huxley (GBH) equation is a prototype model that describes the interplay among reaction, convection, and diffusion. In this article, we explore the controllability of this model by means of an interior control supported in an arbitrary non-empty open subset of the domain. We establish that the GBH equation is not globally approximately controllable in a given time. However, it is possible to steer the system from any steady state to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of another steady state in some suitable time by means of a localized interior control, provided that both steady states lie in the same connected component of the set of steady states.

</details>


### [177] [Random Reshuffling Dominates Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32005)
*Zijian Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次证明，在光滑凸优化中，随机重排（RR）在任何合理步长和任意有限轮数下均优于传统随机梯度下降（SGD），解决了长期存在的理论难题。研究揭示了现有RR收敛理论的两个关键缺陷：步长需小于1/n阈值，以及理论最优率在轮数小于n阈值时劣于SGD。这些限制严重偏离实践，而本文的工作彻底弥合了理论与实际之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机重排（RR）在实践中表现优异，但其收敛理论存在两个长期未解决的缺陷：（1）现有理论要求步长小于与数据量n成反比的阈值；（2）当训练轮数小于n的某个阈值时，RR的理论最优收敛率严格劣于传统SGD。这些限制严重偏离了实际使用场景，使得现有理论无法解释RR在实际中的优越性。作者旨在解决这一开放性问题，为RR优于SGD提供严格的理论证明。

Method: 作者通过理论分析，首次证明了在光滑凸优化中，对于任意合理的步长和任意有限轮数，随机重排（RR）的收敛性能始终优于传统随机梯度下降（SGD）。该证明摆脱了现有理论中对步长（需小于O(1/n)）和轮数（需大于O(n)）的约束限制，得到了更普适且符合实际情形的理论结果。

Result: 作者成功证明了RR在光滑凸优化中，在任何合理步长和任意有限轮数下都严格优于SGD，彻底解决了这一长期悬而未决的理论问题，弥合了理论与实践之间的差距。

Conclusion: 本文填补了随机重排（RR）收敛理论的关键空白，证明了其相对于传统SGD的普适优势，为实践中广泛使用的RR策略提供了坚实的理论基础，推动了优化理论的发展。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{SGD}$) is one of the most classical optimization algorithms with favorable theoretical guarantees, yet the practical implementation of $\textsf{SGD}$ differs subtly from its well-known form and is often referred to as Shuffling Stochastic Gradient Descent ($\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$). A particularly popular strategy in $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ is Random Reshuffling ($\textsf{RR}$), which has achieved great empirical success across numerous experiments. Despite its strong performance, $\textsf{RR}$ has long been considered a heuristic due to a lack of theoretical support. Over the last decade, people have finally established provable convergence rates for $\textsf{RR}$, thus justifying its observed superiority. However, for smooth convex optimization, two clouds over the convergence theory of $\textsf{RR}$ remain to this day. More precisely, according to the current theory, $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ under $\textsf{RR}$ converges only when the stepsize is smaller than a threshold proportional to $1/n$, where $n$ is the number of summands in the objective (or the number of data points). Consequently, the optimally tuned theoretical rate of $\textsf{Shuffling SGD}$ under $\textsf{RR}$ is strictly worse than that of $\textsf{SGD}$ when the number of epochs is smaller than another threshold proportional to $n$. These two restrictions heavily limit the applicability of existing theories and leave a critical mismatch with practice. In this work, for the first time, we prove that $\textsf{RR}$ dominates $\textsf{SGD}$ in smooth convex optimization under any reasonable stepsize after any finite number of epochs, thereby addressing a longstanding open question.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Separation Capacity of Scattering Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30822)
*Konstantin Häberle,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过Cover函数计数理论，分析了卷积神经网络（尤其是散射网络）作为特征提取器的分离能力，建立了新的公式化表述，识别了影响分离容量的关键因素，并提供了网络设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强对CNN作为特征提取器在分类任务中的理论理解，特别是通过Cover函数计数理论来分析其分离能力。

Method: 扩展Cover框架，建立分离容量的新公式；利用该公式分析散射网络中影响分离容量的因素；提供散射网络设计的实践洞见。

Result: 提出了分离容量的新公式化表述，识别了散射网络中影响分离容量的关键因素（网络构建模块层面）。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上深化了对CNN（尤其是散射网络）分离能力的理解，并为网络设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we attempt to enhance the theoretical understanding of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) as feature extractors in classification tasks by analyzing them through the lens of Cover's function-counting theory. Specifically, our focus lies on the notion of separation capacity, a combinatorial quantity derived from counting the number of realizable dichotomies (i.e., binary label assignments). Our contributions are threefold. First, we extend Cover's framework by establishing a conceptually insightful and practically useful formulation for the separation capacity. Second, leveraging this formulation, we identify the factors governing the separation capacity of feature extractors that employ a specific CNN architecture, so-called scattering networks, in terms of their network building blocks. Third, we provide practical insights for scattering network design.

</details>


### [179] [Dynamic Prediction of Alternating Recurrent Events via Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30889)
*Abigail Loe,Susan Murry,Zhenke Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对交替复发事件（如触发不应期的特定事件）开发了一种在线动态预测框架，通过神经网络理论与逆概率加权伪观测值方法，在模拟和医疗住院医师低情绪期预测中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替复发事件广泛存在于行为科学、刑事司法和生物统计等领域，其分析面临相关观测、重复结局及潜在删失等统计挑战，现有方法缺乏有效的动态预测框架。

Method: 面向统计受众开发神经网络理论，结合逆概率加权伪观测值（inverse probability weighted pseudo-observations），构建在线动态预测框架。

Result: 模拟实验表现良好；应用于预测一年级住院医师低情绪期（alternating recurrent event-free time）展现出色能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的在线动态预测框架能有效预测交替复发事件，在医学等实际领域具有应用价值。

Abstract: Alternating recurrent events -- event-times of a specific nature that trigger a secondary refractory period -- occur in a wide-range of fields, including behavioral science, criminal justice, and biostatistics. Analysis of these events requires careful attention to the statistical nuance, including correlated observations and repeated outcomes subject to potential censoring. We develop an online dynamic prediction framework appropriate for predicting subsequent alternating recurrent events, by developing neural network theory for a statistical audiences and applying inverse probability weighted pseudo-observations. The proposed model is applied to dynamically predict alternating recurrent event-free time, showing good performance in simulation, and outstanding capability in application to predicting periods of low mood for first-year medical residents. We close with a discussion.

</details>


### [180] [SGD at the Edge of Stability: Stochastic Stabilization with Large Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30930)
*Konstantinos Emmanouilidis,Lachlan MacDonald,Salma Tarmoun,Rene Vidal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机梯度下降（SGD）在多类交叉熵损失下的收敛性，证明SGD会在边缘稳定性与稳定状态之间交替，但能自我稳定并保证在最佳迭代意义下收敛，即使使用大学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化理论无法解释现代深度学习中使用远大于理论指导的学习率却仍能收敛的现象（边缘稳定性现象），而大多数现有分析局限于确定性梯度下降，随机情形下的边缘稳定性机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对线性分类器和两层神经网络，从理论上分析SGD在多类交叉熵损失下的动力学行为，刻画边缘稳定性与稳定状态之间的交替过程，并证明SGD的自稳定性质。

Result: SGD的随机性导致动力学在曲率驱动的振荡主导的边缘稳定性状态和期望损失可控下降的稳定状态之间交替；但SGD能够自我稳定，保证在固定步数内返回稳定状态，并在最佳迭代意义下收敛。

Conclusion: SGD在大步长机制下具有自稳定能力和收敛保证，实验验证了理论发现并展示了SGD在大学习率下的优势。

Abstract: Modern deep learning has been shown to operate at the edge of stability, routinely using learning rates far larger than those justified by classical optimization theory. Most prior analyses of the edge of stability phenomenon focus on deterministic gradient descent, leaving the stochastic setting largely unexplored. In this work, we provide sharp convergence guarantees for Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) applied to the multiclass cross-entropy loss, for both linear classifiers and two-layer neural networks. We show that the stochasticity of SGD may cause the dynamics to alternate between an edge-of-stability regime that is dominated by curvature-driven oscillations, and a stable regime in which the expected loss decreases at a controlled rate. Despite that, we prove that SGD self-stabilizes the dynamics, ensuring that the iterates return to stability in a fixed number of iterations and allowing convergence in the best-iterate sense even with large learning rates. Experiments validate our theoretical findings and illustrate the benefits of SGD in the large-stepsize regime.

</details>


### [181] [Dynamic Gaussian Processes and the Vanilla-SPDE Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31063)
*Rui-Yang Zhang,Lachlan Astfalck,Edward Cripps,David Leslie,Henry Moss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Vanilla-SPDE Exchange方法，利用标准GP推断与SPDE公式之间的等价性构建混合方案，以降低时空高斯过程推断的计算复杂度，尤其在观测位置与预测位置不重合时改善计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程推断的立方计算成本在时空稠密网格场景下尤为突出，SPDE方法虽在时间维度实现线性复杂度，但空间推断仍为立方复杂度，且观测位置与预测位置不重合时问题更严重。

Method: 利用标准GP推断与SPDE公式间的等价性，构建Vanilla-SPDE Exchange混合方案，通过复杂度分析和数值实验验证性能提升。

Result: 所提混合方案在计算复杂度上优于传统方法，数值实验验证了计算增益。

Conclusion: Vanilla-SPDE Exchange能有效降低时空GP推断的计算瓶颈，尤其适用于观测与预测位置不重合的场景。

Abstract: Gaussian process inference is often limited by cubic computational costs, a challenge that becomes more pronounced in spatio-temporal settings where posterior inference is required over dense grids. While state-space SPDE formulations enable linear complexity in time, exact inference remains cubic in space and deteriorates further when observation locations are disjoint from the prediction locations, which inflates the number of considered spatial points. To address this, we propose the Vanilla-SPDE Exchange, which exploits an equivalence between the standard and SPDE formulations of GP inference to construct a hybrid scheme with improved computational cost. We demonstrate these gains through complexity analysis and numerical experiments.

</details>


### [182] [MNAR-$k$-means: A $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Not at Random with Magnitude-Decaying Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31253)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对MNAR机制下的缺失数据，本文提出了一种基于插补值大小约束的k-means聚类方法，通过约束插补值的幅度来减少聚类中心估计偏差，并证明了该方法的统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的k-means聚类无法直接应用于含有缺失值的不完整数据。对于非随机缺失（MNAR）数据，由于缺失与数据值本身相关，基于均值插补的k-means方法会导致聚类中心估计失真，聚类效果差。而MNAR机制在现实中非常普遍，因此需要改进基于k-means的聚类方法。

Method: 提出一种基于插补值大小约束的新型k-means聚类方法，针对数据在绝对值较小的位置更容易缺失的幅度衰减MNAR场景，约束插补值的大小，具有良好的数学解释性，并采用交替最小化算法求解优化问题。

Result: 模拟实验验证了所提方法在改善聚类效果和减少聚类中心估计偏差方面的有效性；实际缺失数据应用进一步说明了其实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于插补值大小约束的k-means方法能够有效改善MNAR缺失数据下的聚类性能，并且所估计的聚类中心具有统计一致性，能够收敛到完全观测数据的真实聚类中心。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering, based on distances computed from all data features, cannot be directly applied to incomplete data with missing values. A natural extension of $k$-means to missing data is to involve only the observed positions in clustering, which is equivalent to imputing missing values by corresponding cluster means. However, for data missing not at random (MNAR), since missingness is related to data values, such a mean-imputation-based method may lead to the distortion of estimated cluster centers, resulting in a poor clustering result. Since MNAR mechanisms are very common in reality, it is necessary to improve the performance of $k$-means-based clustering methods for such data. In this paper, we focus on a magnitude-decaying MNAR scenario where data is more likely to be missing at positions with smaller absolute values, and we propose a novel $k$-means clustering method based on the constraint of the size of imputation values, which enjoys a good mathematical interpretation. Moreover, we establish the statistical consistency of the estimated cluster centers of the proposed method to the true cluster centers of fully observed data, and solve the optimization of the proposed loss function via an alternative minimization algorithm. Simulation experiments verify the effect of the proposed method in improving clustering results and reducing the bias of estimated cluster centers. Applications to real-world missing data further show the utility of the proposed method.

</details>


### [183] [Accelerating Conformal Prediction via Approximate Leave-One-Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31915)
*Jiachen Cong,Jingbo Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过引入近似留一法（ALO）估计量来加速共形预测，在保持渐近覆盖率和效率的同时显著降低计算成本，并通过模拟实验验证了该方法与精确方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测在不确定性量化方面具有通用性，但其计算成本高昂，尤其是需要对所有观测点进行留一法重新拟合，限制了其在实际中的应用。

Method: 将近似留一法（ALO）估计量融入共形预测框架，替代精确的留一法重新拟合。在高维统计中已有的ALO交叉验证风险估计量一致性分析方法基础上，针对共形预测中需要对新样本点$x_{n+1}$而非训练协变量$x_i$的留一残差这一特点进行适配。

Result: 理论上建立了ALO-based方法的渐近覆盖率和效率保证；模拟实验表明该方法在覆盖率和效率上与精确方法相当，同时显著缩短了运行时间。

Conclusion: ALO估计量能有效加速共形预测，在保持统计性能的前提下大幅降低计算负担，为大规模应用中不确定性量化提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: While conformal prediction provides a general framework for uncertainty quantification in predictive inference, its application is often limited by computational cost. Recent methods, including Jackknife+ and Jackknife-minmax, achieve faster computation by trading a slight loss of efficiency relative to full conformal prediction, but still requires computing leave-one-out refits for all observations. In this paper, we further accelerate conformal prediction by incorporating approximate leave-one-out (ALO) estimators, and establish asymptotic coverage and efficiency. While our proof draws on methods developed for analyzing the consistency of ALO cross-validation risk estimators in high-dimensional statistics, it requires adaptations to handle conformal prediction, where leave-$i$-out residuals are needed for predictions at $x_{n+1}$ rather than just at the training covariate $x_i$. Simulation results validate our theoretical findings, showing that the ALO-based methods achieve coverage and efficiency comparable to the exact methods, while significantly reducing the runtime.

</details>
